The system predicts future content requests using machine learning algorithms and estimates content popularity at the cluster level. Based on these predictions, cache placement decisions are made to maximize efficiency. The simulation results show that the proposed approach performs well under various network conditions, achieving a cache utilization rate of nearly 97% the highest among the methods compared. In addition, it offers an improved hit rate with an acceptable execution time, resulting in reduced backhaul traffic and enhanced user experience.

</details>


### [107] [Dot-Flik: A Scalable Edge AI Architecture for Distributed Insect Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26121)
*Mattia Consani,Denisa-Andreea Constantinescu,Åse Håtveit,Titus Venverloo,Fabio Duarte,Carlo Ratti,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低成本、可扩展的昆虫监测系统，通过边缘端运动过滤算法和分层IoT架构，实现了60-80%的帧率削减和实时监测，为城市生物多样性监测提供了实用解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球昆虫种群数量下降亟需可扩展、持续的监测系统，但现有基于视觉的解决方案受限于高昂的硬件成本、能源需求，以及对集中处理或云连接的依赖。

Method: 提出三个贡献：(1)基于时域差分、伽马校正运动放大和块运动密度分析的运动感知帧过滤算法，在边缘端丢弃无关帧；(2)分布式分层IoT架构，通过边缘预处理将数据采集与AI分类解耦；(3)在低成本商用硬件上进行真实户外部署验证。

Result: 在微风条件下实现60-80%的帧率削减；维持30 FPS实时运行，计算余量为12.8ms；节能高达22.6%；每个中心节点支持5-6个并发边缘流。

Conclusion: 该系统为城市环境中部署密集、低成本的生物多样性监测网络奠定了实用基础。

Abstract: Global insect population declines necessitate scalable, continuous monitoring systems, yet existing vision-based solutions remain constrained by high hardware costs, energy demands, and reliance on centralized processing or cloud connectivity. This article presents three contributions to address these limitations. First, we propose a motion-informed frame filtering algorithm based on temporal differencing, gamma-corrected motion amplification, and block-based motion density analysis that discards irrelevant frames at the edge while preserving insect activity, without requiring deep learning inference on the sensing device. Second, we introduce a distributed, hierarchical IoT architecture that decouples data acquisition from AI classification through this edge-level preprocessing, projecting fractional scaling of central processing requirements and significantly increasing monitoring coverage compared to monolithic single-stream approaches. Third, we validate the complete system through real-world outdoor deployments on low-cost commodity hardware along four axes: real-time performance, network scalability, hardware cost, and energy efficiency under varying wind conditions. Results demonstrate 60-80% frame reduction under light-wind conditions, sustained real-time 30 FPS operation with 12.8 ms of computational headroom, up to 22.6% energy savings, and support for 5-6 concurrent edge streams per central node. These findings establish a practical foundation for dense, low-cost biodiversity monitoring networks in urban environments.

</details>


### [108] [Privacy-Aware Agent Collaboration for Dynamic VR Slice Management in 6G SD-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26123)
*Khaled M. Naguib,Soumaya Cherkaoui,Mahmoud M. Elmesalawy,Ahmed M. Abd El-Haleem,Ibrahim I. Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体强化学习（MARL）的VR切片管理框架，通过融合移动性预测与信息瓶颈编码器，实现了在保护用户隐私前提下的高效资源分配，在吞吐量与隐私保护方面均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络中的虚拟现实（VR）服务对超低时延和高吞吐量有严格要求，这给软件定义无线接入网（SD-RAN）的动态资源管理带来了关键挑战，亟需一种既能高效分配资源又能保护用户隐私的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一种移动性驱动且隐私感知的多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架用于VR切片管理。该框架利用协作智能体在端到端VR链路上最大化资源分配，并引入移动性预测和信息瓶颈编码器，以实现高效且安全的智能体协作。

Result: 仿真对比实验表明，该方法相比传统方法实现了高达34%的吞吐量提升、减少了28%的资源消耗，并将隐私泄露降低了85%。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效保障未来6G环境中沉浸式VR体验的可靠性，在提升网络性能的同时实现了用户隐私保护，为6G VR服务的切片管理提供了一种有效的技术方案。

Abstract: Ultra-low latency and high throughput are required for Virtual Reality (VR) services in 6G networks, which presents critical challenges for Software-Defined Radio Access Networks (SD-RANs) dynamic resource management. This work propose a mobility-driven, privacy-aware Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework for VR slice management, in which cooperative agents maximize resource distribution over end-to-end VR links while protecting the privacy of user data. Our approach incorporates mobility prediction and an information bottleneck encoder to facilitate effective and secure agent collaboration. In simulations, comparisons with traditional methods are studied which show up to 34\% throughput improvement, 28\% fewer resources, and 85\% less privacy leakage, guaranteeing dependable immersive VR experiences in future 6G environments.

</details>


### [109] [Enhancing FANET Routing Resilience: A Fuzzy-Driven Bio-Inspired Approach and Its Quantitative Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26124)
*Xinwang Yuan,Jinshu Su,Yusheng Xia,Congxi Song*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对无人机自组网中的高动态性问题，提出了改进的蜂群聚类算法、双层信标机制、模糊逻辑自适应Hello间隔控制器以及路由弹性评估框架，有效提升了簇结构稳定性并降低了控制开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在飞行自组网中，无人机的高移动性导致网络拓扑快速变化和链路不稳定；现有的聚类方法难以在高度动态环境中维持稳定的簇结构；缩短Hello间隔虽能及时捕获链路变化，但会显著增加控制开销。

Method: 1）采用多因子优化改进基于人工蜂群的簇头选举算法；2）引入双层信标机制进行簇维护；3）提出基于模糊逻辑的Hello间隔控制器，根据网络动态性自适应调整控制消息频率；4）提出参数敏感性指标来定量评估路由弹性。

Result: 在NS-3仿真中与常见基准方案对比，所提协议在整体性能方面表现出显著优势；弹性评估框架分析揭示了峰值性能与参数稳定性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该研究有效解决了飞行自组网中的簇稳定性维护和控制开销平衡问题，为大规模高动态场景下的路由协议设计与评估提供了新的方法论和理论依据。

Abstract: In flying ad hoc networks, the high mobility of unmanned aerial vehicles leads to rapid topology changes and unstable links. Clustering can simplify topology management, yet maintaining stable cluster structures remains challenging in highly dynamic environments. This paper employs multi-factor optimization to enhance the artificial bee colony-based cluster head election algorithm and introduces a dual-layer beacon mechanism for cluster maintenance, thereby improving cluster stability and reducing reconfiguration overhead. Meanwhile, although shortening the hello interval helps capture link variations in a timely manner, it significantly increases control overhead. To address this issue, a fuzzy logic-based hello interval controller is proposed to adaptively adjust control message frequency according to network dynamics, effectively reducing overhead while preserving transmission performance. Furthermore, to quantitatively evaluate routing resilience in large-scale and highly dynamic scenarios, this paper proposes a parameter sensitivity index to characterize both overall protocol performance and its sensitivity to environmental variations. The proposed protocol is compared with common baseline schemes in NS-3 simulations, and the resilience evaluation framework analysis reveals significant advantages in overall performance while exposing the trade-off between peak performance and parametric stability.

</details>


### [110] [Geometric Fairness-Aware Routing for Federated Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26125)
*Ratun Rahman*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出Geo-FairFed，一种结合双曲图神经网络(HGNNs)和联邦优化的几何公平感知路由系统，在6G和边缘智能网络中实现了低延迟、低能耗和高公平性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴6G和边缘智能网络需要在空间分布式设备间实现有效且均衡的路由算法。现有联邦路由系统通常优先考虑聚合延迟或吞吐量，而忽视了公平性和网络拓扑的底层几何结构。

Method: 提出Geo-FairFed系统，每个节点在负曲率流形上学习拓扑感知表示（包含层次关系和连接不对称性），全局聚合器使用曲率正则化目标函数，结合路由损失、几何不一致性和基于Jain公平指数的不平等惩罚来强制执行公平性。

Result: 在动态6G边缘和IoT拓扑上的大量仿真表明，与最先进的联邦和几何路由协议相比，Geo-FairFed平均延迟降低20%，能耗减少17%，公平性提高达21%。理论分析证明了在有限曲率下的收敛保证，并表明所提出的公平性术语在路由性能上产生帕累托改进均衡。

Conclusion: 在双曲流形中嵌入拓扑并将公平性纳入联邦更新，可以显著提高大规模网络路由的效率和公平性。

Abstract: Emerging 6G and edge-intelligent networks require effective and balanced routing algorithms among varied and spatially distributed devices. Existing federated routing systems often prioritize aggregate latency or throughput above fairness and the underlying geometric structure of network topologies. This paper describes Geo-FairFed, a geometric fairness-aware routing system that blends hyperbolic graph neural networks (HGNNs) and federated optimization to provide equal performance across edge nodes. Each node learns topology-aware representations on a negatively curved manifold, which include hierarchical relationships and connection asymmetries. A global aggregator next enforces fairness using a curvature-regularized aim that minimizes routing loss, geometric inconsistency, and an inequality penalty based on Jain's fairness index. A theoretical analysis develops convergence guarantees under limited curvature and shows that the proposed fairness term results in a Pareto-improving equilibrium in routing performance. Extensive simulations on dynamic 6G-edge and IoT topologies reveal that Geo-FairFed minimizes average latency by 20\%, reduces energy consumption by 17\%, and improves fairness by up to 21\% when compared to state-of-the-art federated and geometric routing protocols. The study found that embedding topology in a hyperbolic manifold and including fairness into federated updates can significantly enhance the efficiency and equity of large-scale network routing.

</details>


### [111] [HALO: Hierarchical Auction-assisted Learning for Offloading in SAGIN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26293)
*Xuli Cai,Poonam Lohan,Sachin Ravikant Trankatwar,Burak Kantarci*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出HALO框架，一种用于空间-空中-地面集成网络（SAGIN）的分层拍卖辅助学习方法，在任务卸载和资源调度方面显著优于传统DRL基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在由IoT设备、UAV边缘节点和高空平台站（HAPS）组成的三层SAGIN网络中，时延敏感的任务卸载和资源调度问题面临挑战。该问题被建模为非凸混合整数非线性规划（MINLP）问题，具有NP-hard特性，需要捕获细粒度的系统动态特性。

Method: 提出宏-微时隙模型以跟踪随时间变化的累积传输和计算进度；基于此模型设计HALO框架，结合基于拍卖的任务关联机制与分层近端策略优化（HPPO）算法进行资源分配（包括带宽、发射功率和CPU频率分配）。

Result: 仿真结果表明，HALO在不同流量负载下均优于代表性深度强化学习基线方法。相比PPO平均任务成功率提升8.7个百分点（相对增益11.4%）；相比DDPG和SAC分别实现32.4%和89.9%的相对改进，展现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HALO能够在不同流量条件下保持稳定高效的性能，非常适合时延敏感的SAGIN环境，为三层网络架构中的任务卸载和资源调度提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate delay-aware task offloading and resource scheduling in a three-tier space-air-ground integrated network (SAGIN) consisting of IoT devices, UAV edge nodes, and a high-altitude platform station (HAPS). We formulate joint task association and continuous resource control (including bandwidth, transmit power, and CPU frequency allocation) as a non-convex mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem, which is inherently NP-hard. To capture fine-grained system dynamics, we introduce a macro-micro slot model that tracks cumulative transmission and computation progress over time. Based on this model, we propose HALO, a hierarchical auction-assisted learning framework that combines auction-based task association with hierarchical Proximal Policy Optimization (HPPO) for resource allocation. Simulation results under different traffic loads show that HALO consistently outperforms representative deep reinforcement learning (DRL) baselines. In particular, HALO achieves an average improvement of 8.7 percentage points in task success rate over PPO (corresponding to an 11.4% relative gain) and shows consistently greater robustness than DDPG and SAC, with relative improvements of 32.4% and 89.9%, respectively. These results highlight HALO's ability to maintain stable and efficient performance under varying traffic conditions, making it well-suited for delay-sensitive SAGIN environments.

</details>


### [112] [5G NR-V2X Scheduling Approaches for CPM Variable Size Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26746)
*Vittorio Todisco,Mattia Andreani,Maria Luisa Merani,Alessandro Bazzi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了NR-V2X Mode 2在CPM报文大小波动场景下的调度策略，发现资源分配稳定性比瞬时负载优化更重要，基于填充的SPS方案表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPM报文由于动态目标包含和周期性安全开销导致包大小显著波动，而NR-V2X Mode 2采用的半持续调度（SPS）原本针对周期性、包大小相对稳定的流量设计，现实CPM包大小波动对分布式资源分配的影响尚不明确。

Method: 基于真实感知数据集重构变长CPM流量，对NR-V2X Mode 2调度策略进行系统级比较评估，分析了动态调度和多种SPS方案，包括基于填充的分配、激进的大小驱动重选以及MCS自适应。

Result: 包大小波动在调度稳定性受损时会显著降低可靠性；动态调度和激进重选因频繁资源重分配增加碰撞概率；基于填充的SPS收敛至稳定资源分配并提供鲁棒性能；MCS自适应实现最高平均包接收率但不同包类型间可靠性不均匀。

Conclusion: 在现实CPM流量下，资源使用稳定性比瞬时负载优化更为关键，为NR-V2X Mode 2上的CPS部署提供了设计指导。

Abstract: Collective perception messages (CPMs) introduce significant packet size variability due to dynamic object inclusion and periodic security overhead. While 5G NR-V2X Mode 2 typically employs semi-persistent scheduling (SPS) designed for periodic traffic with relatively stable packet sizes, the impact of realistic CPM-driven size fluctuations on distributed resource allocation remains insufficiently understood. This paper presents a comparative system-level evaluation of NR-V2X Mode 2 scheduling strategies under variable-size CPM traffic reconstructed from real-world perception datasets. We analyze dynamic scheduling and multiple SPS-based approaches, including padding-based allocation, aggressive size-driven reselection, and modulation and coding scheme (MCS) adaptation. Results show that packet size variability can significantly degrade reliability when scheduling stability is compromised. In particular, dynamic scheduling and aggressive reselection increase collision probability due to frequent resource reallocations. In contrast, SPS with padding converges to a stable resource allocation and provides robust performance, while MCS adaptation achieves the highest average packet reception ratio but with uneven reliability across packet types. The findings demonstrate that, under realistic CPM traffic, stability of resource usage is more critical than instantaneous load optimization, and provide design guidelines for CPS deployment over NR-V2X Mode 2.

</details>


### [113] [An Experimental Assessment of the Spatial and Frequency Selectivity of Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26808)
*Cyrille Morin,Leonardo S Cardoso,Maxime Guillaud,Ahmad Shokair,Youssef Nasser,Amelie Hennequart,Geoffroy Lerosey*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过实验研究了可重构智能表面(RIS)对非目标链路的影响，发现即使在标称频率范围之外或使用不同载波频率，RIS配置优化仍会对次级链路产生显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注RIS配置优化对目标通信链路的性能提升，但缺乏对RIS如何影响其优化目标之外的其他无线链路的系统性研究，这一盲区可能对多用户和多系统共存场景带来干扰问题。

Method: 在FR1频段使用CorteXlab无线测试平台和Greenerwave RIS设备进行实验研究。设置三种场景：主链路与次级链路在同一地点、工作于相同频率、或工作于不同频率，评估RIS对次级链路的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明：RIS对次级链路的接收功率和信道相位有显著影响，且这种影响在其标称频率范围之外依然存在；主次链路之间的载波频率分离无法有效缓解这种干扰影响。

Conclusion: RIS对非目标链路的影响不可忽视，在多系统共存场景中需要在RIS配置优化时考虑对周边链路的潜在干扰，这对RIS的实际部署和系统设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: This work investigates the impact of reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) on radio links other than the one for which the RIS configuration is optimized. We consider three different scenarios in which a secondary communication link could be affected by a RIS whose configuration is optimized for a primary communication link operating in the vicinity, on the same or on different frequencies. This question is investigated experimentally in the FR1 band, using the CorteXlab radio testbed and a Greenerwave RIS. We show that the impact, in terms of received power and impact on the channel phase of the secondary link, is significant even outside of the nominal frequency range of the RIS, and is not mitigated by carrier frequency separation between the two communication links.

</details>


### [114] [Toward Agentic SysAdmin: Rethinking System Administration with AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26960)
*Gianmaria Frigo,Davide Saladino,Alberto Castagnaro,Francesco Marchiori,Denis Donadel,Luca Pajola,Mauro Conti*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了NetLLMeval框架，通过实时网络模拟自动评估LLM在网络管理任务中的表现，发现求解器设计对准确性影响巨大，可使14B开源模型正确率从0.43提升至0.88，并在合适配置下匹配万亿参数前沿系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机网络复杂性日益增加（云原生架构、异构设备、分布式系统），网络管理员面临配置、故障排查和安全管理等多重压力。LLM作为辅助工具前景广阔，但现有基准测试依赖静态参考输出或人工专家验证，无法扩展到真实网络状态的多样性和LLM部署策略的多样性。

Method: 提出NetLLMeval框架，利用实时网络模拟自动获取基准真值，无需人工干预。进行全因子研究，涵盖24000次实验运行，包括10个基础模型、4种求解器架构、10种任务类型和6种不同复杂度的网络拓扑。

Result: 求解器设计对准确性影响显著，可将14B开源权重模型正确率从0.43提升至0.88；在合适配置下，本地部署模型能够匹配万亿参数前沿系统性能。

Conclusion: NetLLMeval框架已开源发布，支持未来模型和求解器设计的可重复基准测试，证明了求解器架构优化的重要性以及小规模模型达到大规模前沿系统性能的可行性。

Abstract: The growing complexity of computer networks, driven by cloud-native architectures, heterogeneous devices, and distributed systems, places increasing pressure on network administrators who must simultaneously manage configuration, troubleshooting, and security under tight operational constraints. Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as a promising tool to assist and partially automate these tasks, yet their systematic evaluation in networking scenarios remains an open challenge. Existing benchmarks rely on static reference outputs or manual expert validation, neither of which scales to the diversity of real-world network states or to the variety of orchestration strategies -- from monolithic prompting to fully agentic pipelines --through which LLMs are increasingly deployed.
  In this paper, we present NetLLMeval, a framework for automatically evaluating LLM-based systems on network administration tasks by leveraging live network emulation to derive ground truth without human intervention. Through a full-factorial study of 24000 runs spanning 10 foundation models, 4 solver architectures, 10 task types, and 6 network topologies of increasing complexity, we show that solver design has a great impact on accuracy -- lifting a 14B open-weight model from 0.43 to 0.88 correctness -- and that such locally-deployable models can match trillion-parameter frontier systems under the right configuration. NetLLMeval is released open-source to support reproducible benchmarking of future models and solver designs.

</details>


### [115] [Maximum Achievable Burst Size in All-Optical Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27050)
*Thomas Roethig,Sleman Mouammar,Ítalo Brasileiro,Soheil Hosseini,Admela Jukan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文分析了全光卫星网络在不同星座配置下的最大突发数据包大小，发现在100 Gbps上行链路容量下，基于WDM的光突发交换网络在高轨LEO星座可支持高达500 MB的突发大小，在低轨LEO星座可支持600 MB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究全光卫星网络在不同星座配置下能够实现的最大突发数据包大小，以评估光突发交换技术在卫星通信网络中的性能潜力和适用性。

Method: 基于WDM（波分复用）的光突发交换网络架构，在100 Gbps上行链路容量条件下，对不同高度的低地球轨道（LEO）星座进行理论分析和性能评估。

Result: 在高轨LEO星座中，最大突发大小可达500 MB；在低轨LEO星座中，最大突发大小可达600 MB。

Conclusion: 全光卫星网络中的光突发交换技术能够支持大容量数据传输，且低轨LEO星座相比高轨LEO星座具有更大的突发数据包承载能力，为卫星光通信网络的设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We analyze the maximum burst size achievable in all-optical satellite networks across different constellations. With a 100 Gbps uplink capacity, a WDM-based optical burst switching network supports burst sizes of up to 500 MB in high-altitude LEO constellations and 600 MB in low-altitude LEO constellations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Detecting and Controlling Sycophancy with Cascading Linear Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26155)
*Maty Bohacek,Rishub Jain,Nicholas Dufour,Thomas Leung,Chris Bregler,Roma Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种迭代数据生成流程，通过构建具有线性递进特征的样本对，成功分离出语言模型中负责谄媚行为的线性特征子空间，实现了优于LLM-as-a-judge基线的检测与控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活导向方法需要大量明确表现出期望或不期望行为的对比样本对，这些数据对决定了可解释性框架检测模型特征的可靠性和导向模型行为的能力。现有方法依赖简单的二元样本对，难以解耦复杂的行为特征。

Method: 提出迭代数据生成流程，超越简单二元样本对，分离出表现出与行为呈线性缩放关系的特征程度的样本。专注于检测和引导远离谄媚行为（语言模型优先考虑用户验证的倾向），通过级联样本发现可线性分离的子空间特征。

Result: 通过级联样本发现的谄媚特征形成线性可分离的子空间，能够选择比基线方法更清晰对应期望行为的模型激活。在检测、确定性评分和稳健导向能力上匹配或优于LLM-as-a-judge和系统提示基线，同时具有更低的计算需求和更好的可解释性保证。

Conclusion: 证明了利用级联线性特征样本的方法在激活导向任务中的有效性，为模型行为控制提供了新的数据构建范式，在计算效率和可解释性方面展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Interpreting and controlling model behaviors through activation steering methods requires many pairs of contrastive samples that clearly exhibit desired or undesired behavior. These data pairs determine the degree to which interpretability frameworks can reliably detect model features responsible for a behavior, and therefore the ability to steer models toward or away from such behavior. In this work, we present an iterative data generation pipeline that isolates cascading linear features responsible for a behavior. Specifically, we show how moving beyond simple binary pairs of samples, and instead isolating samples that show degrees of features that scale linearly with behavior, allows for better disentanglement of features. We focus on detecting and steering away from sycophancy -- the tendency of language models to prioritize user validation. We demonstrate that sycophancy features discovered through cascading samples form linearly separable subspaces, and allow for selection of model activations that more clearly correspond to the desired behavior than baseline approaches. We also evaluate their ability to enable detection, deterministic scoring, and robust steering, and see that they either match or outperform LLM-as-a-judge and system prompting baselines while providing lower computational demand and more interpretability guarantees. Code & Data: https://cascading-feats.github.io/

</details>


### [117] [Agentic Analysis for Agentic Infrastructure: An LLM-Powered Pipeline for Comparative Governance of DAO and Corporate AI Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26203)
*Yutian Wang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于LLM的大规模治理话语分析流程，通过对ERC-8004（无许可链上）和Google A2A（企业主导）两种AI智能体互操作性标准的比较研究，发现治理形式虽影响议题焦点，但两种机制均呈现相似的参与不平等和社区碎片化，而无许可治理可能促进更大的主题趋同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体协议的激增，塑造其互操作性标准的治理结构在实证研究方面仍存在显著空白。现有研究缺乏对不同治理模式下话语权力结构和社区参与模式的系统性比较分析。

Method: 研究构建了一个LLM驱动的比较分析流程，整合了自动化标注、神经主题建模和多层网络分析技术。该方法应用于两个对比鲜明的标准：ERC-8004（无许可、链上治理）和Google A2A（企业主导治理），共分析了4,323条治理参与记录，结合LLM辅助编码、主题建模和多层网络分析考察制度设计如何塑造议题优先级和社区结构。

Result: 研究发现治理形式会影响实质性关注焦点，但两种治理机制都表现出相当的参与不平等程度和社区碎片化。话语对齐密度在无许可设置中更高，表明开放治理尽管参与分散，但可能促进更大的主题趋同。

Conclusion: 研究展示了LLM辅助方法如何推进技术治理的实证研究，为设计更公平的智能体AI标准提供了启示。该方法框架可扩展应用于其他技术治理场景，所有数据和代码均已开放获取。

Abstract: As AI agent protocols proliferate, the governance structures shaping their interoperability standards remain empirically underexamined. We introduce an LLM-powered comparative pipeline for large-scale governance discourse analysis, integrating automated annotation, neural topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to study socio-technical power structures at scale. We validate it on two contrasting standards for agent interoperability: ERC-8004 (permissionless, on-chain) and Google A2A (corporate-led). Analyzing 4,323 governance participation records, we combine LLM-assisted coding, topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to examine how institutional design shapes thematic priorities and community structure. We find that while governance form influences substantive focus, both regimes exhibit comparable levels of participation inequality and community fragmentation. Discourse alignment is denser in the permissionless setting, suggesting that open governance may foster greater thematic convergence despite decentralized participation. These findings illustrate how LLM-assisted methods can advance the empirical study of technology governance, with implications for designing more equitable agentic AI standards. All data and code are openly available.

</details>


### [118] [Life After Benchmark Saturation: A Case Study of CORE-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26158)
*Nitya Nadgir,Sayash Kapoor,Kangheng Liu,Peter Kirgis,Matilda Orona,Stephan Rabanser,Tilman Bayer,Abhishek Shetty,Yue Ling,Derrick Chan-Sew,Rumi Nakagawa,Saiteja Utpala,Zachary S. Siegel,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出当基准测试准确率饱和时，不应直接退役，而应评估其他六个关键维度。以CORE-Bench Hard为案例，研究结构有效性、分布外泛化性、效率、可靠性、模型与脚手架的相对重要性以及人机协作的提升，构建了改进版CORE-Bench v1.1和OOD任务集，发现即使准确率饱和，仍可从其他维度获得有意义的性能洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基准测试评估范式过于以准确率为中心，当准确率饱和时便将基准退役，这错过了研究智能体其他关键性能维度的机会。作者认为应该关注六个被忽视的维度：结构有效性问题（如捷径）、分布外泛化能力、效率、可靠性、模型与脚手架的相对重要性，以及人机协作带来的提升。

Method: 以CORE-Bench Hard（科学代码计算可复现性基准）为案例研究：1）识别结构有效性威胁并构建改进版CORE-Bench v1.1和分布外任务集CORE-Bench OOD；2）在准确率饱和后测量效率、可靠性、模型性能和脚手架性能；3）开展小规模随机对照实验，测量真实世界计算可复现性任务中人机协作的提升效果。

Result: 1）发现了CORE-Bench Hard中难以被较弱智能体提前识别的结构有效性威胁；2）即使准确率饱和，CORE-Bench v1.1仍可有效测量效率、可靠性、模型和脚手架性能；3）人机协作实验显示约2倍的统计学显著加速（可能被低估，因为1/5的纯人类复现在时间限制内未完成）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的六维度评估框架为当前以准确率为主导的评估范式提供了更严谨的替代方案。研究表明，即使基准测试准确率饱和，通过评估结构有效性、分布外泛化、效率、可靠性、模型/脚手架相对重要性和人机协作提升等维度，仍可获得对智能体性能的有意义洞察，从而延长基准测试的生命周期并深化对智能体能力的理解。

Abstract: When a benchmark's accuracy saturates, it is often retired and replaced with a more challenging version. We show that this approach privileges accuracy and misses the opportunity to study six other key dimensions of agent performance: construct validity issues such as shortcuts, out-of-distribution generalizability, efficiency, reliability, the relative importance of the model versus the scaffold, and uplift from human-agent collaboration. We use CORE-Bench Hard, a benchmark for computational reproducibility of scientific code, as a case study to demonstrate that measuring agents along these dimensions yields meaningful insights into agent performance even after accuracy saturates. First, we surface threats to construct validity in CORE-Bench Hard that are difficult to anticipate with less capable agents. We introduce an improved benchmark, CORE-Bench v1.1, and an out-of-distribution task suite, CORE-Bench OOD. Second, we find that despite accuracy saturation, CORE-Bench v1.1 remains useful for measuring efficiency, reliability, model performance, and scaffold performance. Finally, we conduct a small-scale randomized experiment to measure uplift from human-agent collaboration on real-world computational reproducibility tasks. We find a statistically significant speedup by about a factor of two -- likely underestimated due to one-fifth of human-only reproductions reaching the time limit before completing -- and describe various other findings. Together, our contributions present a more rigorous alternative to the dominant accuracy-centric evaluation paradigm.

</details>


### [119] [Refusal Lives Downstream of Persona in Chat Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26161)
*Viola Zhong,Qirui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示了指令微调模型中合规人设方向与拒绝方向之间的交互机制：合规人设方向能够门控拒绝方向的表达，从而显著降低模型的拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将拒绝行为与人设特征视为独立机制分别研究，忽视了二者之间可能存在的交互关系。本文旨在探索这两种机制是否相互影响，以及人设特征如何影响模型的拒绝行为。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型中，提取合规人设方向和拒绝方向，通过对两个方向进行干预实验（包括方向引导、拒绝方向重新引入、投影消除等操作），验证二者之间的因果关系。

Result: 合规人设引导显著抑制拒绝行为——在Llama模型中拒绝率从97%降至2%。重新引入拒绝方向仅在晚期层部分恢复拒绝行为。在晚期层窗口投影消除人设方向可恢复拒绝率至基准水平，而投影消除随机方向无此效果。

Conclusion: 拒绝行为在晚期层的表达阶段被门控，位于其计算阶段的下游。将拒绝视为单一孤立方向会忽略其对合规人设的依赖关系，这为理解大模型的安全机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: Linear directions in activation space have been identified for both refusal and persona traits in instruction-tuned chat models, but the two have been studied as separate mechanisms. We show they interact: a compliant persona gates refusal. In Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, we extract a compliant model-persona direction and a refusal direction and intervene on both. Compliant persona steering suppresses refusal -- in Llama, the refusal rate falls from 97% to 2%. Reintroducing the refusal direction partially restores refusal at late layers but not at early ones. Projecting out the persona direction in a late-layer window restores it to baseline; projecting out a random direction does not. Refusal is therefore gated at the late-layer expression stage, downstream of where it is computed. Treating refusal as a single isolated direction misses its dependence on persona.

</details>


### [120] [Instruction Bleed: Cross-Module Interference in Prompt-Composed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26356)
*Ching-Yu Lin,Yifan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现并定义了“组合行为泄漏”(CBL)：在提示词组合的智能体系统中，编辑一个提示词模块会隐式改变其他模块的行为。这种干扰源于Transformer自注意力机制缺乏模块间正式边界。通过三通道协议实验验证，内容通道产生了可检测的配对效应(Cohen's d=0.63)，但未达到改变最终输出的阈值——这种亚阈值效应在标准质量保证中不可见，但会在大量决策中累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从业者报告了一个反复出现的故障模式：编辑一个提示词模块会隐式改变其他模块的行为，尽管它们之间没有共享变量或可执行依赖关系。这种模块间的干扰行为缺乏形式化定义和系统化研究，需要建立理论框架和测量方法。

Method: 1. 将CBL形式化定义为：共享上下文窗口的模块之间的干扰
2. 识别其根本原因：架构非隔离性——Transformer自注意力机制在拼接模块间没有提供正式边界
3. 设计可复用的三通道协议（体量、内容、形式）来扰动非焦点模块
4. 在已部署的职位评估智能体上进行实验（Claude Sonnet 4.6，144次试验）

Result: 仅内容通道产生可检测的配对效应（Cohen's d = 0.63，Bootstrap 95%置信区间不包含零）；没有推荐结果发生翻转——这是一种亚阈值效应，标准QA无法检测，但会在智能体成千上万次决策中累积。CBL与已知智能体故障轴（对抗性注入、认知退化、多智能体故障传播、隐私泄漏）正交。

Conclusion: 建立了CBL的操作性定义、可复用协议、可证伪预测集和系统级特征描述，证明了跨模块干扰测量应成为提示词组合智能体评估的必要要求。

Abstract: Practitioners of prompt-composed agentic systems report a recurring failure mode: editing one prompt module silently shifts the behavior of others despite no shared variable or executable dependency. We formalize this as compositional behavioral leakage (CBL): interference between modules sharing a context window. CBL is enabled by architectural non-isolation: transformer self-attention provides no formal boundary between concatenated modules. We probe CBL on a deployed job-evaluation agent (Claude Sonnet 4.6, 144 trials) through a reusable three-channel protocol that perturbs non-focal modules along volume, content, and form. Only the content channel produces a detectable paired effect (Cohen's d = 0.63, bootstrap 95% CI excluding zero); no recommendation flipped -- a sub-threshold regime invisible to standard QA but compounding across the thousands of decisions a deployed agent makes. CBL is orthogonal to known agent-failure axes (adversarial injection, cognitive degradation, multi-agent fault propagation, privacy leakage). We contribute an operational definition, a reusable protocol, a falsifiable prediction set, and a system-class characterization, establishing cross-module interference measurement as a requirement for prompt-composed agent evaluation.

</details>


### [121] [AlgoEvolve: LLM-driven Meta-evolution of Algorithmic Trading Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26173)
*Dhruv Sharma,Gautam Shroff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AlgoEvolve框架，利用大语言模型作为语义变异算子，通过进化算法自动发现和优化算法交易策略，并引入元进化外循环来改进搜索启发式规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型进化编程应用主要集中于静态编程基准测试，缺乏在动态复杂环境中的应用。算法交易领域具有噪声大、非平稳性和高度不连续性等独特挑战，亟需能够持续适应环境变化的程序合成方法。

Method: 提出了AlgoEvolve框架，将大语言模型作为语义变异算子进行进化搜索：1)内循环生成、评估和迭代改进可执行的Python交易策略；2)引入元进化外循环进化指导程序合成的提示词，自动发现改进的搜索启发式规则。采用严格的测试协议评估策略性能。

Result: 系统展现出涌现的机制适应性策略逻辑，能够自主调整交易规则；元进化外循环发现的启发式规则在平衡探索与利用的同时减少了零交易失败，一致性地优于初始人工设计的指令。

Conclusion: 基于大语言模型的语义进化为复杂环境中的持续程序合成提供了可行方法，证明了该方法在算法交易等动态复杂领域的有效性。

Abstract: Recent work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can act as semantic mutation operators for the evolutionary discovery of programs and proofs. Most current applications focus on static coding benchmarks. We extend this paradigm to algorithmic trading. This domain is uniquely challenging because it is noisy, non-stationary, and highly discontinuous. We present AlgoEvolve, an LLM-driven evolutionary framework that generates, evaluates, and iteratively improves executable trading strategies. These strategies are expressed as Python code and evaluated through a rigorous testing protocol. Across multiple experiments, the system exhibits emergent regime-adaptive strategy logic, including autonomous shifts in trading rules. We further introduce a meta-evolutionary outer loop that evolves the prompts guiding program synthesis in the inner loop. This outer loop discovers improved search heuristics. These heuristics balance exploration and exploitation while reducing zero-trade failures. They consistently outperform initial human-designed instructions. The results demonstrate that LLM-based semantic evolution provides a viable approach for continual program synthesis in complex environments.

</details>


### [122] [Scientific discovery as meta-optimization: a combinatorial optimization case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26728)
*Yuan-Hang Zhang,Chesson Sipling,Massimiliano Di Ventra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了元优化框架，通过LLM生成的目标函数进行相关加权投票，实现评估标准的自我修正和演化，在3-SAT问题上将基线复杂度从N²·⁵¹降至N¹·³³，最大测试实例加速67倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现本质上是一个优化问题，LLMs已能自动探索理论实验的状态空间，但作者认为同时修改评估标准同样重要，因此提出将研究形式化为元优化问题，即优化目标本身也在被优化。

Method: 提出共识目标聚合方法：通过相关加权投票组合LLM生成的目标函数，构建稳定、自修正的评估标准，该标准随理解深入而演化。将此框架应用于基于数字MemComputing机器的3-SAT算法发现问题。

Result: 在3-SAT问题上，将基于问题规模N的基线扩展从约N^2.51降至约N^1.33，在最大测试实例上实现约67倍加速。

Conclusion: 作为一个与问题无关的通用框架，该方法有望显著助力科学发现过程。

Abstract: Scientific discovery is fundamentally an optimization problem, defined by a vast "state space" of theories and experiments, and an evaluation criterion based on quality, novelty, and validity. Large language models (LLMs) have enabled automated exploration of this space, but we argue that simultaneous modification of the evaluation criteria is equally important. Here, we propose formalizing research as meta-optimization, where the optimization objective itself is also being optimized. Our key contribution is "consensus objective aggregation," where LLM-generated objective functions are combined via correlation-weighted voting, yielding a stable, self-correcting evaluation criterion that evolves as understanding deepens. We apply this framework to algorithm discovery for 3-SAT problems based on digital MemComputing machines, reducing the baseline scaling with problem size $N$ from $\sim N^{2.51}$ to $\sim N^{1.33}$ and delivering a $\sim 67\times$ speedup on the largest instances tested. As a problem-agnostic framework, we hope this approach will considerably aid scientific discovery.

</details>


### [123] [Knowledge-augmented Agentic AI for Mental Health Medication Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26205)
*Huizi Yu,Jian Liu,Wenkong Wang,Lingyao Li,Jiayan Zhou,Zhaoqian Xue,Xiang Li,Xinxin Lin,Zhiying Liang,Zhuoru Wu,Siyuan Ma,Xin Ma,Lizhou Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于知识图谱的多智能体框架，成功整合了Reddit、WebMD和FDA三个来源的精神药物安全信息，实现了证据与经验的分离溯源，发现了社区数据作为独立安全信号的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者日益在线寻求药物信息，但精神药物安全知识分裂为权威但抽象的监管不良事件记录和经验贴近但未验证的患者叙述。在精神病学中，信息整合不当可能加剧恐惧、反安慰剂效应和治疗不依从，因此需要一种既能整合多源信息又能保持证据溯源的方法。

Method: 开发了溯源感知的知识图谱多智能体框架，整合了九种抗抑郁药的466,525条Reddit帖子、60,782条WebMD评论和20年FDA不良事件报告系统记录。采用大语言模型实体识别流水线（经医生标注基准测试，药物F1=0.969，疾病F1=0.973），构建基于ATC-N、ICD-10和MedDRA词汇表的Neo4j知识图谱以保持溯源。

Result: 两个社区平台之间高度一致（Jaccard相似度高达0.905），但与监管报告一致性较低，表明患者生成数据形成部分独立的安全信号。对于舍曲林，许多不良事件在社区来源中比FDA记录早出现数百天。知识图谱成功保持了监管事实与患者经验的可追溯性分离。

Conclusion: 源感知整合为更可审计的精神药物信息提供了可行路径，建立了将权威证据与患者经验结合同时保持各自溯源性的方法，其有用性和患者获益有待前瞻性测试。

Abstract: Patients increasingly seek medication information online, yet safety knowledge for psychiatric drugs is split between regulatory adverse-event records, which are authoritative but abstract, and patient narratives, which are experience-near but unvalidated. Integrating them without conflating evidence and anecdote is especially consequential in psychiatry, where poorly contextualised information can amplify fear, nocebo responses, and non-adherence. Here we develop a provenance-aware, knowledge-graph-based multi-agent framework unifying 466,525 Reddit posts, 60,782 WebMD reviews, and twenty years of U.S. FDA Adverse Event Reporting System records for nine antidepressants. A large-language-model entity-recognition pipeline benchmarked against physician annotations reached highest F1 scores of 0.969 for medications and 0.973 for conditions. The two community platforms were far more concordant with each other (overlap up to a Jaccard similarity of 0.905) than with regulatory reports, indicating that patient-generated data form a partly independent safety signal. For sertraline, many adverse events appeared in community sources hundreds of days before the corresponding FDA date. A Neo4j knowledge graph grounded in ATC-N, ICD-10, and MedDRA vocabularies preserves provenance, keeping every claim traceable and regulatory facts distinct from patient experience. These results establish source-aware integration as a route to more auditable psychiatric medication information, with usefulness and patient benefit to be tested prospectively.

</details>


### [124] [Semantic Early-Stopping for Iterative LLM Agent Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27009)
*Sahil Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体LLM循环中固定迭代次数终止机制的问题，提出语义早停方法，在保证质量的前提下减少了38%的运行token消耗。同时发现，相较于"何时停止"，"选择哪一轮最优"才是真正开放的关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体大语言模型（如Writer-Critic循环）普遍采用固定迭代次数上限作为终止条件，这种语法层面的终止机制忽视了答案质量的动态变化：既在简单输入上过度消耗token，又在困难输入上过早截断，无法实现效率与质量的最优平衡。

Method: 提出语义早停机制：当连续草稿的语义嵌入不再发生意义变化（基于余弦距离和耐心窗口）且答案质量停止提升时终止循环。研究做出三大贡献：(1)建立严谨的理论基础，证明确定性终止和良定义性，并将距离序列收敛作为经验验证的猜想；(2)设计高效的评估协议，生成完整轨迹后回放各停止策略，缓存LLM评判调用，分离运行token与评估token；(3)在HotpotQA多跳检索增强问答数据集上进行60问题的实证研究。

Result: 在测试集上，无评判器的语义停止器在质量持平条件下（Delta-IS = -0.004, p = 0.81）相对于固定迭代次数减少38%的运行token消耗；完整质量门控变体因每轮评判成本过高而适得其反；能选择最优轮次的oracle相比所有实用策略获得+0.115的信息分数（p ~ 4e-11）。

Conclusion: 语义早停能有效降低多智能体LLM循环的token消耗，但研究揭示核心问题应从"何时停止"（已解决）重新定义为"哪一轮最优"（仍是开放问题），为后续研究指明方向。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) loops, for example a Writer that drafts and a Critic that revises, are almost always terminated by a fixed iteration cap (max_iterations). This is a syntactic kill-switch: it is blind to whether the answer is still improving, so it over-spends tokens on easy inputs and truncates hard ones. We study semantic early-stopping: the loop halts when consecutive draft embeddings stop changing in meaning (cosine distance with a patience window) and the answer's measured quality stops improving. Our work makes three contributions. First, an honest theoretical footing: we prove deterministic termination and well-definedness and machine-check these claims, while treating the convergence of the distance sequence as an empirically tested conjecture rather than a (previously over-claimed) Banach contraction. Second, a judge-efficient evaluation protocol: we generate each question's full trajectory once, replay every stopping policy over the identical drafts, and cache every LLM-judge call, yielding a strictly paired efficiency-versus-quality comparison at low cost; we further separate operational tokens (charged to a policy) from evaluation tokens (a measurement instrument). Third, an empirical study on multi-hop retrieval-augmented question answering (HotpotQA). On the 60-question test split, a judge-free semantic stopper reduces operational tokens by 38% relative to max_iterations at parity quality (Delta-IS = -0.004, p = 0.81), whereas the full quality-gated variant is counter-productive because its per-round judging dominates cost. An oracle that selects the best round attains +0.115 Information Score over every practical policy (p ~ 4e-11), reframing the problem from "when to stop" (easy) to "which round is best" (open).

</details>


### [125] [Accelerating Skill Assessment in Chess: A Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26267)
*Tianyuan Zhou,Zhizheng Fu,Tianming Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DD-Elo评级系统，通过引入漂移扩散模型整合走棋级别的数据，解决了传统Elo系统响应滞后的问题，实现了更快适应技能变化的同时保持与原系统的理论一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Elo评级系统仅依赖比赛结果，存在响应滞后问题，无法捕捉细粒度的游戏质量。虽然引入逐走棋信息可以改善这一问题，但游戏状态空间的巨大和数据的噪声带来了显著挑战。

Method: 受认知神经科学中的漂移扩散模型(DDM)启发，提出DD-Elo评级系统。该方法将技能表达建模为决策过程，整合走棋级别的数据以捕捉快速的技能波动，并提供严格的数学推导证明其与传统Elo系统保持有界偏差。

Result: 大量实验证明DD-Elo比传统Elo系统更快适应技能变化，同时保持理论上的向后兼容性。系统在响应速度上有显著提升。

Conclusion: DD-Elo为国际象棋评级生态系统提供了一个可解释、高响应性和向后兼容的解决方案，有效平衡了响应速度与系统稳定性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Rating systems such as Elo serve as the gold standard for matchmaking in competitive chess. However, they inherently suffer from response lag due to their exclusive reliance on match outcomes, neglecting the granular quality of gameplay. Nevertheless, incorporating move-by-move information into rating adjustments presents a significant challenge given the substantial noise and the vastness of the game-state space. To address this, we propose the Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System (DD-Elo), a novel skill assessment framework inspired by the drift diffusion model (DDM) from cognitive neuroscience. By modeling skill expression as a decision-making process, our model integrates move-level data to capture rapid skill fluctuations. We provide a rigorous mathematical derivation proving that DD-Elo maintains a bounded deviation from the traditional Elo system, ensuring theoretical alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DD-Elo adapts to skill changes faster than Elo. Our findings suggest that DD-Elo offers an explainable, highly responsive, and backward-compatible solution for chess rating ecosystems. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/Aquila-zhou1/DD-Elo .

</details>


### [126] [Governing Actions, Not Agents: Institutional Attestation as a Governance Model for Autonomous AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26298)
*Jakob Salfeld-Nebgen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于高风险AI智能体系统的计算治理模型，要求在执行关键行动时提供独立的认证证据，而非监控推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主AI智能体开始执行临床开药和生产软件部署等具有重大后果且不可逆转的行动，需要建立有效的治理机制。观察到人类机构在管理强大的自主行动者时，并非通过监控其推理过程，而是要求在关键行动执行时提供独立认证的证据。

Method: 提出一种计算治理模型：智能体在规划和推理方面保持完全自主，但对指定的高风险行动不拥有执行权限。执行取决于预设条件，这些条件由独立的权威来源认证、密码学绑定到声明意图，并由确定性策略评估。决策记录在可独立重新验证的防篡改日志中。

Result: 提出了概念验证实现，并通过软件部署和临床开药的案例展示了该模型的应用。

Conclusion: 该模型为自主AI智能体系统提供了一种有效的治理模式，通过独立认证机制确保高风险行动的安全性，同时保持智能体的自主推理能力。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents may begin to perform consequential, irreversible actions such as clinical prescribing and production software deployment. This paper observes that human institutions have governed powerful autonomous actors not by monitoring their reasoning but by requiring independently attested evidence at the point of consequential action. We formalise this institutional pattern as a computational governance model for AI agent systems. Under the proposed model, an agent retains full autonomy over planning and reasoning but holds no execution authority over designated high-risk actions. Execution is conditional on preconditions that are each independently attested by a separate authoritative source, cryptographically bound to a declared intent, and evaluated by a deterministic policy. Decisions are recorded in a tamper-evident log amenable to independent re-verification. We present a proof-of-concept implementation and illustrate the model with examples from software deployment and clinical prescribing.

</details>


### [127] [COrigami: An AI Pipeline for Co-Designing Flat-Foldable Visually Recognisable Origami](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26299)
*Tom Zahavy,Shaobo Hou,Thomas Tumiel,James Doran,Francesco Faccio,Xidong Feng,Alex Havrilla,Igor Khytryi,Chenglei Li,Lisa Schut,Vivek Veeriah,Arijan Abrashi,Michał Kosmulski,Robert J. Lang,Nick Robinson,Brandon Wong,Marcus Chiam,Gloria Fang,Satinder Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了COrigami，一个端到端的AI驱动管道，通过自然语言生成折纸折痕图，结合算法优化和自主审美评估，在计算折纸领域实现几何约束与视觉美感的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式AI在具有可验证解的问题上取得了显著成功，但在生成同时满足严格几何约束和主观视觉美感的物理艺术作品方面仍存在挑战。计算折纸作为一个数学上严格的环境，为艺术设计提供了平折性方程的基础，但缺乏有效的AI辅助设计工具。

Method: 提出了COrigami端到端管道，包含五个关键步骤：1）生成语义火柴人图；2）计算基础填充；3）求解平折性折痕图；4）对平折折痕图进行塑形；5）利用强化学习与自主审美评估循环对生成模型进行优化。系统结合了算法优化与自主审美评判机制。

Result: COrigami系统能够作为高效的协作助手，从自然语言描述生成结构化的折纸折痕图起点，供人类艺术家进一步扩展和塑造。系统成功实现了多目标物理约束的满足，并展示了AI系统在数学基础上的共创能力。

Conclusion: 该工作通过整合算法优化与自主审美评判，展示了AI系统如何满足多目标物理约束，实现可靠、数学基础的共同创造力。这为AI辅助的物理艺术设计提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: While generative AI has achieved remarkable success in solving problems with verifiable solutions, generating physical art that satisfies both strict geometric constraints and subjective visual aesthetics remains a challenge. This paper presents an approach to tackle these difficulties in the domain of computational origami, a mathematically rigid environment that grounds artistic design within the equations of flat foldability. We present COrigami, an end-to-end AI-driven pipeline that assists the design cycle by generating crease patterns from natural language. Our pipeline involves generating a semantic stick figure, computing a base packing, solving for a flat-foldable crease pattern, shaping the flat-folded crease pattern, and refining the generated model using reinforcement learning driven by an autonomous aesthetic evaluation loop. Our system acts as a highly effective collaborative assistant, generating structural starting points that human artists can further expand and shape. By integrating algorithmic optimisation with autonomous aesthetic critique, this work demonstrates how AI systems can satisfy multi-objective physical constraints to enable reliable, mathematically grounded co-creativity.

</details>


### [128] [The Verification Horizon: No Silver Bullet for Coding Agent Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26300)
*Binghai Wang,Chenlong Zhang,Dayiheng Liu,Jiajun Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Mouxiang Chen,Rongyao Fang,Siyuan Zhang,Xuwu Wang,Yuheng Jing,Zeyao Ma,Zeyu Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出在编程智能体领域，验证解决方案比生成解决方案更难。通过三个维度（可扩展性、忠实性、鲁棒性）刻画验证信号质量，研究四种奖励构造方法，发现针对性验证设计能有效抑制奖励黑客行为并提升任务完成质量，且验证器必须与生成器协同进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直觉认为验证解比产生解容易，但在现代编程智能体中这一直觉被颠覆：基础模型推理能力增强，工程利用日益复杂，生成复杂候选解不再困难，可靠地验证它们反而成为更难的问题。验证器只是人类意图的代理，而非意图本身，且意图本质上规格不足，训练时优化会扩大代理与意图的差距，表现为奖励黑客或信号饱和。

Method: 提出从三个维度刻画验证信号质量：可扩展性、忠实性和鲁棒性，论证同时实现三者是核心挑战。研究四种奖励构造：通用编码任务的测试验证器、前端任务的规则验证器、真实世界智能体任务的用户作为验证器、长时域任务的自动化智能体验证器。在不同任务类型和策略能力水平下，对奖励设计的核心挑战及如何更有效利用奖励信号进行深入分析和实验。

Result: 实验表明，针对性验证设计能够有效抑制奖励黑客行为，提高任务完成质量，并在多个内部和公共基准上实现显著提升。核心观察是：没有固定的奖励函数能在策略能力持续增长的情况下保持有效；验证必须与生成器协同进化。

Conclusion: 验证质量需在可扩展性、忠实性、鲁棒性三维度同时达标，这对验证器设计提出了更高要求。随着策略能力提升，固定奖励函数难以维持有效性，验证机制必须与生成器能力协同演进，以适应不断变化的需求和能力边界。

Abstract: A classical intuition holds that verifying a solution is easier than producing one. For today's coding agents, this intuition is being inverted: as foundation models develop stronger reasoning capabilities and engineering harnesses grow more sophisticated, generating complex candidate solutions is no longer difficult -- reliably verifying them has become the harder problem. Every verifier we can build is only a proxy for human intent, never the intent itself. This makes verification subject to a twofold difficulty: first, intent is underspecified by nature, making it inherently hard to faithfully check whether it has been fulfilled; second, during model training, optimization widens the gap between proxy and intent -- manifesting as reward hacking or signal saturation. To address this, we characterize the quality of verification signals along three dimensions -- scalability, faithfulness, and robustness -- and argue that achieving all three simultaneously is the central challenge. We further study four reward constructions: a test verifier for general coding tasks, a rubric verifier for frontend tasks, the user as verifier for real-world agent tasks, and an automated agent verifier for long-horizon tasks. Across different task types and policy capability levels, we conduct in-depth analysis and experiments on the core challenges of reward design and how to more effectively leverage reward signals. Experiments show that targeted verification design can effectively suppress reward hacking, improve task completion quality, and achieve significant gains across multiple internal and public benchmarks. These experiences collectively point to a core observation: no fixed reward function can remain effective as policy capability continues to grow; and verification must co-evolve with the generator.

</details>


### [129] [How Do Tool-Augmented LLM Agents Perform on Real-World Energy Analytics Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26346)
*David Akinpelu,Akintonde Abbas,Rereloluwa Alimi,Ayodeji Lana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对能源领域缺乏动态、多步骤推理评估基准的问题，构建了一个包含243个专家级任务的工具增强型LLM智能体评估环境，对能源市场分析任务进行了系统性实证研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体基准测试主要覆盖通用领域和金融、法律等特定领域，而能源领域的评估仍局限于静态知识回忆。这是一个关键缺口，因为能源领域需要实时数据检索、专业监管和市场知识，以及在现实约束下进行多步骤定量推理。

Method: 构建了一个包含243个专家策划问题的评估环境，涵盖市场数据检索与分析、知识检索与解释、高级定量建模与决策分析三大类别。智能体配备了可配置的领域工具套件，包括美国主要ISO的实时电力市场API、监管档案搜索、公用事业费率数据库、资产优化模型以及能源市场文档的检索增强生成。采用多维评估协议，对方法正确性、答案准确性、属性对齐和来源有效性进行评分，并通过类别感知路由将评分标准与问题类型匹配。

Result: 对闭源和开源LLM进行了比较评估，分析了模型能力与领域工具在高风险专业领域中的交互作用，并公开发布了关键工件以支持可复现性和未来研究。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了能源领域动态评估基准的空白，为工具增强型LLM智能体在复杂专业领域的应用提供了系统性评估框架和基准数据，有助于推动能源市场分析智能体的发展。

Abstract: Agentic benchmarks have emerged across general-purpose and domain-specific settings, including finance, coding, law, and drug discovery, yet energy-domain evaluations remain largely limited to static knowledge recall. This is a critical gap for a sector that requires live data retrieval, specialized regulatory and market knowledge, and multi-step quantitative reasoning under real-world constraints.
  We present an empirical study of tool-augmented LLM agents on real-world energy market analytics tasks. Our evaluation environment includes 243 expert-curated problems across three categories: (1) Market Data Retrieval and Analysis, (2) Knowledge Retrieval and Interpretation, and (3) Advanced Quantitative Modeling and Decision Analytics. Tasks include price and demand analysis, tariff impact modeling, asset revenue and returns estimation, hedging strategy analysis, and optimization modeling, with problems spanning multiple difficulty levels.
  Agents are equipped with a configurable suite of domain tools, including live electricity market APIs for major U.S. ISOs, regulatory docket search, utility tariff databases, asset optimization models, and retrieval-augmented generation over energy market documents. We assess agent responses using a multi-dimensional evaluation protocol that scores approach correctness, answer accuracy, attribute alignment, and source validity, with category-aware routing to match scoring criteria to question type. We evaluate both closed-source and open-source LLMs, providing a comparative analysis of how model capability and domain tooling interact in a high-stakes professional domain. Key artifacts are publicly released to support reproducibility and future research.

</details>


### [130] [What We are Missing in Multimodal LLM Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26348)
*Po-han Li,Shenghui Chen,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的评估方法滞后于模型能力发展，现有基准测试存在局限性，无法有效衡量模型跨模态信息整合能力，并识别出时空连贯性、物理世界理解、多模态一致性和选择性注意力等关键评估空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型能力发展迅速，但评估方法未同步跟进。现有评估基准多局限于孤立任务，无法充分揭示模型是否真正实现了跨模态信息整合，亟需识别当前评估体系的不足与空白。

Method: 系统审视当前多模态大语言模型的评估方法，回顾现有基准分类体系，分析识别评估中存在的空白领域，包括时空连贯性、物理世界理解、多模态一致性和选择性注意力等维度。

Result: 识别出当前多模态大语言模型评估中的关键缺陷与空白，明确了现有基准在衡量模型跨模态整合能力方面的不足，提出了需要重点关注的评估维度。

Conclusion: 填补这些评估空白对于准确衡量多模态智能的真实进展、揭示模型能力边界至关重要，是推动多模态大语言模型发展的关键环节。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can process diverse inputs, e.g., text, images, audio, and video, and generate textual responses. While their capabilities have advanced rapidly, evaluation of such models has not kept pace. Most existing evaluation benchmarks are limited to isolated tasks and reveal little about whether a model integrates information across modalities. We examine current means for evaluating MLLMs and review the existing benchmark taxonomy to identify gaps, including temporal-spatial coherence, physical world understanding, multimodal consistency, and selective attention. Addressing these gaps is essential for measuring real progress in multimodal intelligence and exposing capability boundaries.

</details>


### [131] [OpenFinGym: A Verifiable Multi-Task Gym Environment for Evaluating Quant Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26350)
*Kaicheng Zhang,Wen Ge,Lei Jiang,Weixin Yang,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Jialin Yu,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpenFinGym，一个统一的量化金融智能体开发环境，解决了现有评估平台任务单一、忽视金融相关性的问题，支持预测、市场生成、实时交易和欺诈检测等多阶段工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型智能体在量化金融工作流中的应用评估存在明显不足：评估任务分散且彼此孤立，基准任务常常忽视金融相关性。然而实际金融工作流本质上是多阶段的，包括预测、策略构建、风险管理和交易等相互依赖的任务。现有平台通常只关注单一任务，因此可能夸大智能体的能力，无法揭示其在泛化、真实市场交互和金融决策方面的弱点。

Method: 提出OpenFinGym统一环境框架：(1) 在单一执行和验证接口下覆盖预测、市场生成、实时交易和欺诈检测任务；(2) 提供自动化任务构建管道，将量化金融论文转化为可执行任务包；(3) 容器化运行时配合主机端验证服务，支持可扩展智能体部署并防止运行时训练-测试泄漏；(4) 具有低延迟数据流设计的模拟交易引擎；(5) 支持长期和事件市场预测的延迟解析机制；(6) 集成SFT和RL后训练功能。

Result: OpenFinGym成功构建了一个涵盖量化金融完整工作流的统一评估环境，能够支持多阶段金融任务的协同评估，提供了将学术研究转化为可执行任务的自动化流程，并确保了评估过程的可扩展性和数据完整性。

Conclusion: OpenFinGym填补了量化金融智能体统一评估环境的空白，通过多阶段任务整合、容器化部署和防泄漏机制，为金融智能体的真实能力评估提供了更可靠的平台，有助于推动该领域的规范化发展。

Abstract: Although large language model agents are increasingly applied to quantitative-finance workflows, their evaluation remains fragmented across isolated tasks, while the financial relevance of benchmark tasks is often overlooked. Yet financial workflows are inherently multi-stage, spanning interdependent tasks such as forecasting, strategy construction, risk management, and trading. Existing platforms typically focus on a single task, and can therefore overstate agent competence and fail to reveal weaknesses in generalization, real-market interaction, and financially meaningful decision-making. We introduce OpenFinGym, a unified gym environment for quantitative-finance agent development that covers forecasting, market generation, real-time trading, and fraud detection under a single execution and verification interface. OpenFinGym additionally provides an automated task-construction pipeline that turns quantitative finance publications into executable task packages; a containerised runtime with a host-side verifier service that supports scalable agent rollouts and prevents runtime train-test leakage; a paper trading engine with a low-latency data-stream design; deferred-resolution support for long-horizon and event-market forecasts; and integration for SFT and RL post-training

</details>


### [132] [Accelerating Returns and the Qualitative Engine for Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26359)
*Guojun Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出加速回报理论主要提升执行和基础设施能力，而真正的科学发现依赖于定性推理能力，这是当前AI系统与人类灵活推理之间巨大差距的根源。通过定位定性科学引擎(QES)来弥补这一缺失能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ray Kurzweil的加速回报理论是技术进步讨论中最具影响力的叙事之一，声称多技术领域(计算、人工智能、脑科学、生物技术)的进步相互作用的自放大效应导致近似指数级发展。作者旨在质疑：即使这种加速真实存在，是否能解决科学发现的核心问题？

Method: 首先对加速回报理论进行简单数学解释；然后通过区分执行/基础设施能力与定性推理能力来分析科学发现的本质需求；最后引入定性科学引擎(QES)框架作为对缺失能力的回应方案。

Result: 加速回报理论最能自然应用于执行和基础设施能力，而真正的科学发现依赖于不同的能力——对当前框架何时结构性不足以及下一步需要什么概念性转变的定性推理。最新ARC-AGI-3结果验证了这一区分：人类在该基准测试中达到天花板水平，而前沿AI系统仍低于1%，表明当前AI与人类灵活推理之间的差距仍然巨大。

Conclusion: 加速回报理论有助于解释定量能力为何可能加速，而QES解决了加速本身无法解决的科学发现核心问题。QES的价值不取决于AGI何时到来，而在于科学发现过程本身构成了一种值得保存、组织和传承的人类智慧形式。人类必须保持意义感和生活焦点选择权，AI的未来不仅是技术预测，更是关于哪些形式的人类理解值得保存和传承的问题。

Abstract: Ray Kurzweil described a thesis of accelerating returns, which is the most influential narratives in discussions of technological progress. Its central claim is that advances in multiple technological fields, especially compute, artificial intelligence, brain science, and biotechnology, interact in such a way that progress becomes self-amplifying and approximately exponential. This paper gives a simple mathematical interpretation of that claim and then argues that, even if such acceleration is real, it does not by itself resolve the central problem of scientific discovery. The reason is that accelerating returns apply most naturally to executional and infrastructural capability, whereas genuine discovery often depends on a different capacity: qualitative reasoning about when a current framework is structurally inadequate and what conceptual move is needed next. Recent ARC-AGI-3 results sharpen this distinction: humans solve the benchmark at ceiling, whereas frontier AI systems remain below 1%, indicating that the gap between current AI and human flexible reasoning is still very large. At the same time, Demis Hassabis has emphasized that humans must retain their sense of meaning and what they choose to focus their lives on, a reminder that the future of AI is not only a technical forecast but also a question of what forms of human understanding are worth preserving and transmitting. This paper positions the Qualitative Engine for Science (QES) [3] as a response to that missing capacity. In this view, the Kurzweil theory helps explain why quantitative capability may accelerate, while QES addresses the central problem in scientific discovery that acceleration alone does not solve. Its value does not depend on when AGI arrives, but on the fact that the processes of scientific discovery themselves constitute a form of human wisdom worth preserving, organizing, and making accessible.

</details>


### [133] [Narration-of-Thought: Inference-Time Scaffolding for Defeasible Ethical Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26366)
*Patrick Cooper,Alvaro Velasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了叙述性思维(NoT)方法，通过五个结构化步骤显著改善了AI在道德困境中的推理质量，将利益相关者忽略率从31%降至1%以下，不确定性抑制率从72%降至1-24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准思维链在处理道德困境时存在两个关键失败模式：利益相关者坍缩（推理过程中仅提及至多一方利益相关者）和不确定性抑制（在采取行动前没有明确的未知因素或保留表述）。这些缺陷导致AI系统在道德决策中缺乏透明度和可审计性，阻碍了可信赖的智能代理部署。

Method: 提出叙述性思维框架，将思维链结构化为五个部分：主角、利益相关者、两步后果分析、不确定性评估、最后承诺。该方法不增加训练、参数或微调，仅通过系统提示实现。研究在100个DailyDilemmas场景上测试了来自三个供应商的四个生成器，采用匹配预算的冗长思维链作为对照，进行了消融实验和文本梯度下降优化。

Result: NoT将利益相关者坍缩率从最高31%降至1%以下，将不确定性抑制率从最高72%降至1-24%。在利益相关者计数上获得Cliff's delta优势+0.79至+0.90，在不确定性评分上获得+0.65至+0.93。扩展到五轮多方利益相关者辩论协议时，将6%的僵局转化为95%完全共识，在DailyDilemmas复制实验中实现100%组合收敛。

Conclusion: NoT框架生成的推理痕迹能够外化利益相关者、后果和不确定性，为每个承诺提供可审计的基础，为可信赖的智能代理部署提供了审计支撑。跨族训练评判器（与生成器不同供应商）在所有测量维度上均优于族内评判器。

Abstract: Standard chain-of-thought on moral dilemmas exhibits two failure modes: stakeholder collapse (the trace names at most one party with a stake in the outcome) and uncertainty suppression (no explicit unknowns or hedges before committing to an action). We introduce narration-of-thought (NoT), a system prompt that structures chain-of-thought into five sections: protagonist, stakeholders, two-step consequences, uncertainty, then commitment. NoT adds no training, parameters, or fine-tuning. On 100 DailyDilemmas scenarios across four generators from three vendors, NoT cuts stakeholder collapse from up to 31% to under 1% and uncertainty suppression from up to 72% to 1-24% on every model. A matched-budget verbose-CoT control rules out token spend as the active ingredient; NoT retains Cliff's delta advantages of +0.79 to +0.90 on stakeholder count and +0.65 to +0.93 on uncertainty score for three of four generators, and a section ablation attributes each shift to its specific sub-instruction. Textual-gradient descent initialised at NoT improves the scaffold further; a cross-family training judge (different vendor from the generator) dominates an in-family one on every measured axis. Extended to a five-round multi-stakeholder debate protocol, the scaffold converts a 6% standoff into 95% full consensus on a calibration set and 100% combined convergence on a DailyDilemmas replication. The resulting traces externalise the stakeholders, consequences, and uncertainty grounding each commitment, providing an auditable substrate for dependable agentic deployment.

</details>


### [134] [Geometry-Aware MCTS for Extremal Problems in Combinatorial Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26399)
*Luoning Zhang,Xu Zhuang,Tianhao Wang,Nathan Kaplan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出几何感知蒙特卡洛树搜索框架（Geometry-Aware MCTS），用于解决组合几何中的极值问题，在六个问题中的五个上取得了新的最佳计算结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合几何中的极值问题（如$n \times n$网格中满足严格全局几何约束的点配置）面临组合爆炸的挑战，传统精确求解器难以处理，而标准强化学习和基于Transformer的模型则受限于稀疏奖励"有效性悬崖"和二次token消耗限制。

Method: 提出Geometry-Aware MCTS框架：(1)通过对可行动作空间的增量更新严格强制几何约束，将共线点约束检查复杂度从$O(n^3)$降至$O(n^2)$；(2)利用几何对称性提高搜索效率，包括节点扩展时的规范剪枝（降低分支因子）和对称批转换（加速发现优质配置）。

Result: 在六个测试问题中的五个上建立了新的最佳计算结果：(1)对于Max-N3IL问题，在$82 \le n \le 119$的网格中找到了约$1.8n$大小的配置；(2)对于Smallest Complete Set问题，找到了约$0.95n$大小的配置，为测试网格提供了新的上界。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了Geometry-Aware MCTS作为发现组合几何中新配置的高度适应性框架，能够有效处理严格几何约束并显著提升搜索效率。

Abstract: We study certain extremal problems in combinatorial geometry that ask about configurations of points in an $n \times n$ grid that satisfy strict, global geometric constraints. Classical exact solvers suffer from combinatorial explosion for these types of problems, and standard reinforcement learning and transformer-based models struggle with the sparse reward "validity cliff" and quadratic token-consumption limits. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose a Geometry-Aware Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) framework. Our approach strictly enforces geometric constraints through incremental updates to the feasible action space. For constraints about collections of collinear points, like those that occur in the classic No-Three-in-Line problem (Max-N3IL), this mechanism reduces the constraint checking complexity from $O(n^3)$ to $O(n^2)$. To improve search efficiency, we exploit geometric symmetries in two ways: canonical pruning during node expansion to reduce the branching factor, and symmetric batch transitions to accelerate the discovery of promising configurations. We perform extensive experiments and establish new best-known computational results on five out of six of the problems that we considered. Notably, for Max-N3IL we find configurations of size roughly $1.8 n$ for grids of size $82 \le n \le 119$. For the Smallest Complete Set problem, we find configurations of size roughly $0.95 n$, providing new upper bounds within the tested grids. This work establishes Geometry-Aware MCTS as a highly adaptable framework for discovering novel configurations in combinatorial geometry.

</details>


### [135] [When Agents Meet Electric Bus Fleet Operations: Pricing Behavior, Trade-offs, and Policy Implications in an Aggregator Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26400)
*Jônatas Augusto Manzolli,Ali Eslami,Luis Miranda-Moreno,Jiangbo Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于智能体的聚合器框架，用于协调电动公交车的运营和V2G服务，通过优化核心和监管智能体实现了自适应调度，但揭示了在利润导向配置下可能从公交运营商提取价值的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动公交车队的运营需要协调服务可靠性、电池荷电状态、充电桩可用性、电价、路线能量不确定性和V2G机会等多个异构因素，现有的决策环境复杂且缺乏有效的实时协调机制。智能体系统为连接异构数据源和自动化流程提供了新范式，需要探索其在电动公交V2G运营中的应用潜力。

Method: 提出智能体聚合器框架，将基于优化的电动公交调度模型与监管智能体耦合。优化核心在路线、充电桩、电池和V2G交换之间执行物理可行性约束；智能体层包括扰动检测、电价适应和调度评估三个监管智能体，负责解释变化的运行条件、在需要时触发实时重新优化，并定义灵活性价值在聚合器和公交运营商之间的分配方式。

Result: 在现实的车站案例研究中，评估了日前和实时运营下的利润导向和运营导向协调模式，考虑了服务延误、路线能量偏差、电价冲击和组合干扰。结果表明：智能体聚合能够通过维持可行调度、选择性激活重新优化、改善充电和V2G灵活性利用来支持自适应车队-电网协调。但同时揭示了关键权衡：当围绕利润导向定价配置时，相同的智能体能力可能从公交运营商提取价值。

Conclusion: 智能体聚合器在管理电动公交V2G运营方面具有实用价值，但在公共车队场景中的部署需要透明的协调模式、可审计的电价设定和明确的价值共享规则，以确保各方利益平衡。

Abstract: Agentic systems are changing how complex operational tasks are coordinated, introducing a new paradigm for connecting heterogeneous data sources and automating processes. Electric bus fleets provide a relevant test case. Their operation requires continuous coordination between service reliability, battery state-of-charge, charger availability, electricity prices, route-energy uncertainty, and vehicle-to-grid (V2G) opportunities. This paper proposes an agentic aggregator framework that streamlines this decision environment by coupling an optimization-based electric bus scheduling model with supervisory agents for disturbance detection, tariff adaptation, and schedule evaluation. The optimization core enforces physical feasibility across routes, chargers, batteries, and V2G exchanges, while the agentic layer interprets changing operating conditions, triggers real-time re-optimization when needed, and defines how flexibility value is allocated between the aggregator and the public transport operator (PTO). A realistic depot case study evaluates day-ahead and real-time operations under profit-based and operation-based coordination modes, considering service delays, route-energy deviations, electricity price shocks, and combined disturbances. The results show that agentic aggregation can support adaptive fleet-grid coordination by maintaining feasible schedules, activating re-optimization selectively, and improving the use of charging and V2G flexibility. However, they also reveal a critical trade-off: the same agentic capability that reduces operational complexity can extract value from the PTO when configured around profit-oriented pricing. These findings suggest that agentic aggregators can become useful for managing electric bus V2G operations, but their deployment in public-fleet contexts requires transparent coordination modes, auditable tariff-setting, and explicit value-sharing rules.

</details>


### [136] [Unbiased Canonical Set-Valued Oracles Via Lattice Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26418)
*Jobst Heitzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对非智能体“神谕”AI在预测未来事件时面临的自我指涉问题（即预测结果本身会改变被预测事件的概率），本文提出了一种基于信任集（credal set）的自洽解决方案，利用Knaster-Tarski不动点定理在完备格上定义了唯一的规范性答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非智能体“神谕”AI在估计未来事件概率时面临自我指涉问题：一旦其答案被获知并被采取行动，它可能会改变原本被要求报告的概率。现有的解决方案（如科学家AI计划所倡导的）仅提出反事实问题，但这种答案在被学习的那一刻就变得无关紧要了，因为其前提已经变得虚假。因此需要一种能够同时满足无偏性和自我一致性的替代方案。

Method: 提出神谕AI不应报告单个概率值，而是报告一个信任集（credal set），该信任集同时满足无偏性和与被学习后果的自洽性。为了从众多满足朴素自洽性要求的集合中筛选出唯一的、有意义的规范解，作者利用Knaster-Tarski不动点定理，在闭信任集的完备格上，取适当定义的单调算子的最小不动点；另一种变体则报告包含所有自洽点估计的最小不动点。该方法是纯粹的格论方法。

Result: 证明了规范答案的存在性、自洽性和非空性；证明了该构造对于非执行性问题会塌缩为经典的点答案；证明了对于二元事件，在自然的凸包分解假设下，规范答案是一个区间。该格论方法可直接推广到任意随机变量，将条件概率P(B|A,C)替换为条件分布L(X|A,C)。

Conclusion: 本文成功利用格论和不动点理论为神谕AI的自我指涉问题提供了一套严格的数学框架，给出了规范答案的定义、存在性证明和基本性质。该方法对非执行性问题保持与经典理论的一致性，对二元事件给出了直观的区间解释，并可推广到一般情形。最后提出了包括区间刻画本身在一般化中是否仍然成立等开放性问题。

Abstract: A non-agentic "oracle" AI that estimates probabilities of future events faces a self-reference problem: once its answer is learned and acted upon, it can change the very probability it was asked to report. One response, advocated for the Scientist AI programme, is to ask only counterfactual questions, evaluated as if the answer had no influence. We observe that such answers tend to become irrelevant the moment they are learned, precisely because their premise is then false. We therefore explore a self-referential alternative in which the oracle reports not a single probability but a credal set that is simultaneously unbiased and self-consistent with the consequences of being learned. The naive self-consistency requirement is satisfied by too many sets (including the useless answer $[0,1]$), so the problem is to single out a canonical, nontrivial member. We do so with the Knaster--Tarski fixed-point theorem on the complete lattice of closed credal sets, taking the least fixed point of a suitably defined isotone operator; a variant instead reports the least fixed point that contains every self-consistent point estimate. We prove existence, self-consistency, and nonemptiness, show that the construction collapses to the classical point answer for non-performative questions, and that for a binary event the canonical answer is, under a natural hull-factoring assumption, an interval. The development is purely lattice-theoretic and extends unchanged from a binary event $B$ to an arbitrary random variable $X$, with $P(B\mid A,C)$ replaced by the conditional law $\mathcal{L}(X\mid A,C)$. We close with open questions, including whether the interval characterization itself survives that generalization.

</details>


### [137] [Estimating Uncertainty in Classifier Performance with Applications to Large Language Models and Nested Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26422)
*Kylie Anglin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对社会科学文本分类中性能指标置信区间估计不准确的问题，通过模拟评估发现，Wald区间和基本百分位Bootstrap等默认方法在样本量小、类别稀少及数据嵌套条件下表现不佳，推荐使用Agresti-Coull、Wilson、Clopper-Pearson等方法，并提出一种新颖的伪计数正则化Bootstrap方法，特别是对于F1指标的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者使用文本分类模型测量构念时，通常提供召回率、精确率等性能指标作为效度证据，但这些指标是受抽样变异影响的点估计。当前领域内对不确定性的度量报告不一致，且在小样本或高性能情况下，所使用的方法往往不适当。本文旨在改善和提高该领域置信区间报告的准确性和一致性。

Method: 通过模拟研究，评估在典型社会科学文本分类条件下（小到中等样本量、构念稀有、文本嵌套在个体内）的性能指标置信区间方法。比较多种区间估计方法，包括Wald区间、基本百分位Bootstrap、Agresti-Coull、Wilson、Clopper-Pearson，以及提出的伪计数正则化Bootstrap方法。对于嵌套数据，调整有效样本量和自由度，并比较层级Bootstrap和聚类Bootstrap的表现。

Result: 默认方法如Wald区间和基本百分位Bootstrap准确度最低，覆盖率有时远低于名义95%水平。使用Agresti-Coull、Wilson、Clopper-Pearson及新颖的伪计数正则化Bootstrap（对F1计算尤其相关）能提高准确性。当文本嵌套于个体时，需调整有效样本量和适当自由度以获得准确的分析区间。在Bootstrap方法中，当个体产生中等数量文本时，层级Bootstrap比聚类Bootstrap更准确；当个体仅产生少量文本时则过于保守。

Conclusion: 通过为领域提供合适的区间估计指导，旨在提高机器学习应用的透明度，并鼓励在设计阶段更加关注验证样本量的问题，从而增强文本分类模型评估的可靠性和科学性。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use text classification--supervised models or large language models--to measure constructs from natural language, providing metrics such as recall and precision as evidence of their validity. Yet, though these metrics are point estimates subject to sampling variation, measures of uncertainty are inconsistently reported alongside them. Further, when they are reported, they are often estimated with methods that are not appropriate when relevant labelled datasets are small or performance is high. To increase and improve confidence interval reporting in the field, this paper evaluates confidence interval methods for performance metrics under conditions typical of social science text classification: small to moderate sample sizes, infrequent constructs, and texts nested within individuals. Across simulations, default methods such as the Wald interval and the basic percentile bootstrap are the least accurate, with coverage sometimes far below the nominal 95% level. Accuracy is improved with the use of Agresti-Coull, Wilson, Clopper-Pearson, and a novel pseudo-count regularized bootstrap (which is particularly relevant to the calculation of F1). When texts are nested within individuals, we demonstrate that adjustment for both effective N and the appropriate degrees of freedom is necessary for producing accurate analytic intervals. Among bootstrap intervals, the hierarchical bootstrap is more accurate than the cluster bootstrap when individuals produce a moderate number of texts but overly conservative when individuals produce only a few. By providing guidance to the field on appropriate interval estimation, we aim to improve the transparency of machine learning applications, and to encourage greater attention to the validation sample size at the design stage.

</details>


### [138] [Data-driven Machine Learning Cannot Reach Symbolic-level Logical Reasoning -- The Limit of the Scaling Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26454)
*Tiansi Dong,Mateja Jamnik,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明了监督深度学习在理论上无法达到符号级三段论推理，因为训练数据无法区分所有24种有效三段论类型，且端到端映射会导致神经组件间的矛盾。实验表明Euler Net无法实现严格推理，ChatGPT-5虽可达100%准确率但仍存在错误解释，因此监督学习系统无法达到符号逻辑推理的严密性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面神经网络在无训练数据情况下实现了符号级三段论推理，这引发了一个关键问题：数据驱动的机器学习系统是否可以通过增加训练数据和训练时间达到相同的符号级推理水平？本文旨在探究监督深度学习在逻辑推理方面的局限性，确定其是否能够达到符号级推理的严密性。

Method: 通过理论分析揭示监督深度学习的两个方法论局限：(1)训练数据无法区分所有24种有效三段论推理类型；(2)从前提到结论的端到端映射在模式识别和逻辑推理的神经组件之间引入矛盾的训练目标。实验方面：验证Euler Net无法实现严格三段论推理；测试ChatGPT-5系列模型在四种表面形式（单词、双词、简单符号、长随机符号）下的三段论陈述可满足性判定能力。

Result: 理论分析证明监督深度学习存在无法克服的方法论局限。实验显示：(1)Euler Net无法实现严格的三段论推理；(2)ChatGPT-5系列模型虽然在某些情况下可以达到100%准确率，但仍提供错误解释；(3)表面形式显著影响推理性能。由于训练过程在达到100%准确率后即停止，模型无法学习到真正的逻辑推理规则。

Conclusion: 监督机器学习系统无法达到符号逻辑推理的严密性。这揭示了数据驱动方法在逻辑推理任务中的根本局限，表明单纯增加训练数据和训练时间无法弥合与符号级推理之间的鸿沟，为逻辑推理的缩放定律设定了明确边界。

Abstract: Sphere neural networks have achieved symbolic level syllogistic reasoning without training data, raising the question of where the limit of the scaling law for logical reasoning lies, i.e., whether data-driven machine learning systems can achieve the same level by increasing training data and training time. We show two methodological limitations that prevent supervised deep learning from reaching the symbolic-level syllogistic reasoning: (1) training data can not distinguish all 24 types of valid syllogistic reasoning; (2) end-to-end mapping from premises to conclusion introduces contradictory training targets between neural components for pattern recognition and logical reasoning. Beside theoretical analysis, we experimentally illustrate that Euler Net cannot achieve rigorous syllogistic reasoning. We further challenge the most recent ChatGPTs (GPT-5-nano and GPT-5) to determine the satisfiability of syllogistic statements in four surface forms (patterns): words, double words, simple symbols, and long random symbols, showing that surface forms affect the reasoning performance and that ChatGPT GPT-5 may reach 100% accuracy but still provide incorrect explanations. As empirical training processes are stopped after achieving 100% accuracy, we conclude that supervised machine learning systems will not attain the rigour of symbolic logical reasoning.

</details>


### [139] [MKG-RAG-Bench: Benchmarking Retrieval in Multimodal Knowledge Graph-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26458)
*Xiaochen Wang,Bao Hoang,Han Liu,Ting Wang,Fenglong Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了MKG-RAG-Bench，这是一个跨领域的基准测试，专门用于评估多模态知识图谱检索增强生成（MKG-RAG）中的检索性能。实验表明，有效的多模态检索仍然具有挑战性，但对端到端MKG-RAG性能至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG基准测试在很大程度上忽视了多模态知识图谱RAG（MKG-RAG）中检索的挑战。在实践中，检索是一个关键瓶颈：多模态知识具有异构性，难以跨模态对齐，而且为非结构化语料库设计的检索器往往无法很好地服务于多模态知识。

Method: 作者引入了MKG-RAG-Bench，这是一个跨领域基准测试，由两个跨通用和医疗领域的多模态知识图谱构建，包括精心对齐的问答数据集。该基准测试使用基于LLM的筛选流程构建，过滤低效用知识，生成具有精确监督的结构化查询，并系统地覆盖各种模态配置。

Result: 通过对代表性检索器系列和模态设置进行广泛实验，研究发现有效的多模态检索仍然具有挑战性，但对端到端MKG-RAG性能至关重要，且检索质量强烈决定生成结果。

Conclusion: 通过将检索作为一等评估目标进行隔离，MKG-RAG-Bench为诊断当前局限性和推进多模态知识图谱RAG系统提供了有原则的基础。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over knowledge graphs has emerged as a promising approach for grounding large language models, yet existing benchmarks largely overlook the challenges of retrieval in multimodal knowledge graph RAG (MKG-RAG). In practice, retrieval is a critical bottleneck: multimodal knowledge is heterogeneous, difficult to align across modalities, and often poorly served by retrievers designed for unstructured corpora. To address this gap, we introduce MKG-RAG-Bench, a cross-domain benchmark explicitly designed to evaluate retrieval in MKG-RAG. MKG-RAG-Bench is constructed from two multimodal knowledge graphs spanning general and medical domains, and includes carefully aligned question-answering datasets that support controlled evaluation of both retrieval and downstream generation. The benchmark is built using an LLM-based curation pipeline that filters low-utility knowledge, generates structurally grounded queries with exact supervision, and systematically covers diverse modality configurations. Through extensive experiments across representative retriever families and modality settings, we show that effective multimodal retrieval remains challenging yet crucial for end-to-end MKG-RAG performance, and that retrieval quality strongly determines generation outcomes. By isolating retrieval as a first-class evaluation target, MKG-RAG-Bench provides a principled foundation for diagnosing current limitations and advancing multimodal knowledge graph RAG systems.

</details>


### [140] [auto-psych: Automating the science of mind using agent-driven theory discovery and experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26460)
*Ben Prystawski,Kushin Mukherjee,Daniel Wurgaft,Linas Nasvytis,Michael Y. Li,Noah D. Goodman,Michael C. Frank*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了auto-psych系统，这是一个基于智能体的自动化科学发现系统，能够独立通过众包平台收集人类数据并生成认知科学理论，在人类判断随机性的案例研究中表现优于文献中的理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI科学自动化中数据收集是主要瓶颈。心理学和计算认知科学具有独特优势：理论通常用代码表示，众包平台支持大规模程序化人类数据收集，因此适合实现自动化实验和理论发现。

Method: 开发了auto-psych系统，采用嵌套的智能体发现循环：(1)内循环负责推测、拟合和批判概率认知模型；(2)外循环设计实验、在线发布、收集和分析数据。以硬币翻转序列随机性判断这一经典认知心理学案例为测试平台。

Result: 系统能够通过系统性实验快速可靠地从合成数据中恢复真实理论，嵌套结构对模型性能至关重要。在三轮独立的人类实验中，系统发现的理论比科学文献生成的理论更好地拟合数据。

Conclusion: 证明了在计算认知科学中实现自动化数据收集和理论发现的可行性，为AI驱动的科学自动化提供了有效范例。

Abstract: AI-based scientific automation is increasingly possible by using agents to generate hypotheses, design experiments, and analyze data. Data collection is a major bottleneck in this pipeline, however. Psychology, and computational cognitive science in particular, is well-positioned to benefit from AI experimentation because theories are often represented as code and crowdsourcing platforms enable programmatic human data collection at scale. Here, we apply automated discovery techniques to the project of generating theories in computational cognitive science, with an agent-based system collecting human data independently through crowdsourced survey experiments. As a testbed, we use a classic case study from cognitive psychology: judging which sequences of coin flips seem subjectively more random. Our system, auto-psych, uses nested agent-based discovery loops to generate explanatory theories of human behavior. The inner loop conjectures, fits, and critiques probabilistic cognitive models; the outer loop designs experiments to test these models, launches them online, and analyzes the data. This system can quickly and reliably recover ground-truth theories from synthetic data via systematic experimentation, but the nested structure is critical to model performance. Further, in three independent sequences of human experiments, the system finds theories that fit the data better than theories generated from the scientific literature. This work thus demonstrates the feasibility of automated data collection and theory discovery in computational cognitive science.

</details>


### [141] [Clinical Harness for Governable Medical AI Skill Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26494)
*Tianhan Xu,Lei Bao,Yongxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了临床AI技能和临床线束框架，通过运行时治理架构实现AI临床能力的注册、编排、保护和监控，并以骨质疏松症为例验证了知识驱动、数据驱动和物理增强技能在全生命周期护理中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI仍围绕孤立模型展开，而临床护理需要跨时间的可追溯能力，两者之间存在差距。当前的AI系统缺乏持续性和问责机制，无法满足临床护理对连贯性和责任性的要求。

Method: 提出临床AI技能概念和临床线束框架，这是一个运行时治理架构，用于注册、编排、保护和监控AI驱动的临床能力。以骨质疏松症为示例，展示知识驱动、数据驱动和物理增强三类技能如何在运行时治理下支持全生命周期护理。

Result: 成功构建了临床线束运行时治理架构，实现了AI临床能力的注册、编排、保护和监控功能。通过骨质疏松症案例验证了三类技能（知识驱动、数据驱动、物理增强）能够在统一治理框架下协同支持患者的全生命周期护理。

Conclusion: 临床AI技能和临床线束框架为医学AI从孤立模型转向持续可追溯的临床能力提供了可行路径，运行时治理架构能够确保AI临床应用的安全性、可追溯性和连续性，为临床AI的实际部署提供了新的组织范式。

Abstract: Medical AI remains organized around isolated models, whereas clinical care requires accountable capabilities that persist across time. We propose clinical AI skills and the Clinical Harness: a runtime governance architecture for registering, orchestrating, guarding and monitoring AI-enabled clinical capabilities. Using osteoporosis as an exemplar, we show how knowledge-driven, data-driven and physics-enhanced skills can support lifecycle care under runtime governance.

</details>


### [142] [Humans Disengage, Reasoning Models Persist: Separating Difficulty Registration from Deliberation Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26502)
*Han-yu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型推理模型(LRMs)与人类在问题解决时间上表现出相似的模式，但在错误与正确回答的时间分配上呈现相反趋势：模型在错误时消耗更多token，人类则在错误时花费更少时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型推理模型与人类在问题解决过程中的时间分配策略是否存在深层差异，揭示表面相似性下隐藏的认知机制分歧。

Method: 将推理过程分解为两个层面：跨项目难度追踪(注册)和同项目内失败/成功资源分配；在公开的人类-LRM匹配语料库上比较人类与五种思考型LRM的行为模式，使用项目固定效应分析验证结果的稳健性。

Result: 人类与所有五种思考型LRM在跨项目难度追踪上一致，但在项目内分配上完全相反：LRMs在错误回答上消耗更多token(Cohen's d = 1.47-3.13)，人类则相反；该分歧在多种数据集上重复出现，在非思考型基线模型中不存在。

Conclusion: 人类采用"参与-放弃"策略：对预期可解决的问题投入更多时间；LRMs采用"不确定性驱动"策略：推理链长度随不确定性增长而扩展。两种策略产生相同的跨项目相关性，但反映了根本不同的控制机制。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) take longer on harder problems, just as humans do. This surface similarity hides an opposite pattern within items. When an LRM gets a problem wrong, it spends more tokens than when it gets the same problem right; humans do the reverse, spending less time on the trials they get wrong. We separate two levels of deliberation: how response time tracks difficulty across items (registration), and, with item identity held fixed, whether an agent spends more on its own failures or successes (allocation). On a public matched human-LRM corpus, humans and all five thinking LRMs reproduce the known cross-item alignment (registration) but diverge within items (allocation): every LRM shows a large wrong-vs-right effect (Cohen's d = 1.47-3.13 on H-ARC) while humans show the opposite sign. The comparison stays inside each agent's own scale; we never put seconds and tokens on one axis. The dissociation holds under item fixed effects, replicates across datasets, and is absent in a non-thinking baseline. We read the human pattern as engagement versus abandonment: people stay on items they expect to solve and give up on the rest. We read the LRM pattern as length driven by uncertainty: chains grow when the model is unsure, which is exactly when it tends to fail. Both policies produce the same cross-item correlation with difficulty, so they look aligned on the measure prior work has used; the divergence shows up only once item identity is fixed.
