TL;DR: 本文提出AURORA-AI框架，通过自适应资源编排提升AI系统在动态环境下的性能与公平性，实现快速恢复与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统常部署于非平稳的计算、人口统计及操作条件下，静态资源分配策略在预测性能、公平性和可解释性等方面表现退化，需引入自适应机制保障系统韧性。

Method: 提出AURORA-AI框架，将Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman反馈控制、Lyapunov稳定性监控与公平性感知的复合效用函数统一为闭环策略，持续在异构AI模型群体间重分配计算预算，以最大化综合效用（涵盖预测性能、人口统计公平性、成本、延迟、鲁棒性与可解释性）。

Result: 在注入人口统计偏差冲击、渐进概念漂移与突发黑天鹅事件的离散时间仿真中，AURORA-AI在黑天鹅事件后立即恢复（静态基线需88步、PPO需22步），提升α分位数与超分位数分别约29%和25%，同时降低平均与最大人口统计公平性差距，并增加Lyapunov稳定运行步数比例。

Conclusion: 基于稳定性理论的公平性感知自适应编排是实现韧性、以人为中心AI部署的可行且有理论支撑的路径。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are increasingly deployed under non-stationary computational, demographic, and operational conditions in which static resource allocation strategies degrade both predictive performance and human-centric properties such as fairness and explainability. This paper presents AURORA-AI, an Adaptive Utility-driven Resource Orchestration framework for Resilient AI that unifies Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman feedback control, Lyapunov-based stability monitoring, and a fairness-aware composite utility into a single closed-loop policy.The framework continuously redistributes computational budget across a population of heterogeneous AI models so that the global utility, defined jointly over predictive performance, demographic parity, cost, latency, robustness, and interpretability, remains maximised under disruption. The framework is evaluated in a stress-rich discrete-time simulation that concurrently injects demographic bias shocks, gradual concept drift, and abrupt black-swan disruptions, and is compared against five established controllers including Static, Round Robin, Greedy, LinUCB, and a deep reinforcement-learning agent based on Proximal Policy Optimisation. AURORA-AI achieves immediate recovery from the black-swan event compared to eighty-eight time steps for the Static baseline and twenty-two for Proximal Policy Optimisation, lifts the alpha-quantile and the super-quantile by twenty-nine and twenty-five percent respectively, simultaneously reduces the mean and maximum demographic parity gap, and increases the fraction of Lyapunov-stable operating steps. These results indicate that fairness-aware adaptive orchestration grounded in stability theory is a practical and theoretically motivated path toward resilient human-centric AI deployment.

</details>


### [178] [How to evaluate clustering with ground truth?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27061)
*Pasi Fränti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 推荐使用质心指数(CI)进行聚类评估；若需更精细的点级度量，可根据需求选择配对集合指数(PSI)或聚类准确率(ACC)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对有真实标签可用时的聚类评估问题，本文旨在回顾常见的基于集合匹配的外部有效性指标，并为不同应用场景提供指标选择建议。

Method: 综述并分析常见的外部有效性指标，重点研究基于集合匹配的度量方法，评估其特性和适用条件。

Result: 质心指数(CI)作为直观的聚类级度量被推荐使用；配对集合指数(PSI)提供了不受聚类规模偏差影响的归一化分数；当所有数据点权重相等时，聚类准确率(ACC)等集合匹配度量适用。

Conclusion: 应根据具体需求选择合适的外部聚类评估指标：聚类级分析推荐CI，点级精细分析可选用PSI或ACC，取决于是否需要归一化处理和对聚类规模的敏感性。

Abstract: External indexes can be used for cluster evaluation when ground truth is available. We review the most common external validity indexes focusing on set-matching-based measures. We recommend centroid index (CI), because it is an intuitive cluster-level measure with an explainable result. If we need a more fine-tuned, point-level measure, there are more choices. Pair-set index (PSI) provides a normalized score which is not biased by cluster sizes. If all points should matter equally, then clustering accuracy (ACC) or any other set-matching measure is suitable.

</details>


### [179] [Joint Learning of Experiential Rules and Policies for Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27136)
*Shicheng Ye,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了JERP方法，通过联合学习经验规则池和策略，解决了LLM智能体在多步交互环境中有效利用积累经验的问题，在AlfWorld和WebShop任务上取得了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作在利用交互经验时存在两难选择：将经验作为自然语言规则在外部存储虽易于解释但可能与演进中的策略不同步；使用轨迹反馈更新模型参数虽能广泛改进策略但在稀疏奖励环境下对局部错误的纠正有限。因此需要一种能够同时利用这两种方式优势的方法。

Method: 提出JERP（Joint Learning of Experiential Rules and Policies）框架，包括：1）从相同的交互轨迹中同时更新长期经验规则池和策略；2）在决策时检索任务相关规则，将其与交互历史一起作为条件输入；3）每个回合结束后，利用收集的轨迹既优化策略又通过比较当前rollout与参考成功轨迹来修订规则池，使规则池与演进策略保持对齐。

Result: 在AlfWorld和WebShop两个复杂交互任务基准上的实验表明，JERP在决策性能上取得了持续的提升，验证了联合学习方法的有效性。

Conclusion: JERP通过耦合规则池更新和策略优化，成功解决了经验利用中可解释性与策略同步之间的矛盾，既保持了规则池与策略的一致性，又允许稳定有效的行为逐渐被模型吸收，为LLM智能体的经验学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: For LLM agents in multi-step interactive environments, a key challenge is to make effective use of accumulated interaction experience. Existing work has typically separated two uses of such experience: keeping it outside the model as natural-language rules for later prompting, or using trajectories and feedback to update the model parameters. The former is easy to interpret but can fall out of sync with the evolving policy; the latter improves the policy more broadly but provides only limited correction for local mistakes in sparse-reward settings. We present Joint Learning of Experiential Rules and Policies for LLM Agents (JERP), which updates a long-term experiential-rule pool and the policy from the same interaction trajectories. At decision time, JERP retrieves task-relevant rules and conditions the agent on them together with the interaction history. After each episode, it uses the collected trajectories both to optimize the policy and to revise the rule pool by comparing current rollouts with reference successful trajectories. This coupling keeps the rule pool aligned with the evolving policy while allowing stable and effective behaviors to be gradually absorbed into the model itself. Experiments on AlfWorld and WebShop show that JERP yields consistent gains in decision performance for complex interactive tasks.

</details>


### [180] [OpenRCA 2.0: From Outcome Labels to Causal Process Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27154)
*Aoyang Fang,Yifan Yang,Jin'ao Shang,Qisheng Lu,Junjielung Xu,Rui Wang,Songhan Zhang,Yuzhong Zhang,Boxi Yu,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了PAVE协议构建首个具有细粒度因果传播路径标注的RCA基准OpenRCA 2.0，揭示了现有仅标注根本原因的方法掩盖了LLM代理在因果路径推理上的关键缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RCA数据集仅标注根本原因而缺乏因果传播路径，导致任务退化为简单模式匹配，无法真正评估LLM代理的多步推理和因果理解能力。

Method: 提出PAVE标注协议，利用故障注入中的已知干预信息，采用前向验证机制（从原因到效应）重构因果传播路径，构建包含500个实例、跨系统的细粒度标注基准OpenRCA 2.0。

Result: 在11个前沿LLM上，仅20.7%能准确恢复根本原因集；76.0%能识别至少一个正确根因服务，但仅61.5%能将服务正确锚定到因果传播路径，暴露了"无根据诊断"这一隐藏失败模式。

Conclusion: 仅关注结果的评估方法掩盖了LLM代理的因果推理缺陷，细粒度因果传播路径标注是构建可信LLM-RCA代理的关键缺失环节。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) poses a holistic test of LLM agentic capabilities, such as long-context understanding, multi-step reasoning, and tool use. However, existing datasets suffer from a fundamental gap: they label only the root cause, not the propagation path connecting it to the observed symptom, which largely simplifies the task to naive pattern matching. To support rigorous evaluation, we introduce PAVE, a step-wise labeling protocol that leverages known interventions from fault injection to reconstruct causal propagation paths. The mechanism is forward verification: reasoning from cause to effect rather than inferring backward from symptoms. Applying PAVE yields OpenRCA 2.0 (500 instances), the first cross-system RCA benchmark with step-wise causal annotations for LLM agents. Across 11 frontier LLMs, recovering the exact root-cause set succeeds in only 20.7% of cases on average. To locate where this difficulty lies, we relax the criterion and find what we call the ungrounded diagnosis: agents identify at least one correct root-cause service in 76.0% of cases, but ground that service in a verified causal propagation path to the observed symptom in only 61.5%. Outcome-only evaluation hides this failure mode; step-wise causal ground truth is the missing piece for trustworthy LLM-based RCA agents.

</details>


### [181] [TOPS: First-Principles Visual Token Pruning via Constructing Token Optimal Preservation Sets for Efficient MLLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27161)
*Tinghao Wang,Yichen Guo,Rui Huang,Zheng Lu,Qizhe Zhang,Chenxi Li,Yuan Zhang,Jiajun Cao,Zhirong Shen,Yaosong Du,Guangyan Gan,Wenya Wang,Lin William Cong,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TOPS，一种无需训练、模型无关的视觉token剪枝模块，基于信息论分析确立任务相关性、信息覆盖和语义多样性三大原则。在LLaVA-NeXT上，TOPS可移除77.8%的视觉token，同时保持甚至略微提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)因视觉token数量庞大而面临计算效率瓶颈。现有视觉token剪枝方法存在明显缺陷：基于注意力的标准容易保留冗余token，基于多样性的标准往往忽略用户指令，且组合多种标准的方法缺乏对token剪枝内在目标的原则性表述。

Method: 从第一性原理出发，将视觉token剪枝形式化为构建Token最优保留集(TOPS)。通过自上而下的信息论分析，识别出有效token选择的三个基本原则：任务相关性、信息覆盖和语义多样性。基于这些原则提出TOPS，一种无需训练且模型无关的剪枝模块。

Result: 在7个MLLM骨干网络和14个基准测试上的广泛实验表明，TOPS在各种剪枝设置下均优于现有方法。在LLaVA-NeXT上，TOPS移除了77.8%的视觉token，同时在其7B和13B模型上分别保持了100.0%和100.6%的性能。

Conclusion: 剪枝冗余视觉token有时可以缓解幻觉问题，这为未来轻量级MLLM设计提供了启发。TOPS为视觉token剪枝提供了原则性的理论框架和实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved strong multimodal reasoning capabilities, but their efficiency is limited by the large number of visual tokens, which introduces substantial computational overhead. Visual token pruning offers a natural solution, yet existing methods are imperfect: attention-based criteria tend to retain redundant tokens, while diversity-based criteria are often agnostic to user instructions. Even methods that combine multiple criteria still lack a principled formulation of the intrinsic objective of token pruning. In this paper, we revisit visual token pruning from a first-principles perspective and formulate it as constructing Token Optimal Preservation Sets. Through a top-down information-theoretic analysis, we identify three fundamental principles for effective token selection: Task Relevance, Information Coverage, and Semantic Diversity. Based on these principles, we propose TOPS, a training-free and model-agnostic pruning module that can be applied to various MLLMs. Extensive experiments on 7 MLLM backbones and 14 benchmarks demonstrate that TOPS outperforms prior methods under diverse pruning settings. Notably, on LLaVA-NeXT, TOPS removes 77.8% of visual tokens while preserving 100.0% and 100.6% performance on its 7B and 13B models, respectively, suggesting that pruning redundant visual tokens can sometimes mitigate hallucination and inspire future lightweight MLLM design.

</details>


### [182] [A Process Harness for Uplifting Legacy Workflows to Agentic BPM: Design and Realization in CUGA FLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27188)
*Fabiana Fournier,Lior Limonad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出流程安全带（Process Harness）机制，通过在不替换底层工作流引擎的情况下，将传统工作流升级为代理型业务流程管理（Agentic BPM），实现了确定性流程执行与策略约束的代理自主性的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的确定性工作流引擎难以处理知识密集型任务和动态适应性需求，而完全替换现有引擎成本高昂。因此需要一种机制，既能保留现有引擎的结构化权威和合规性保障，又能引入LLM的推理和适应能力，以满足命令性需求（结构合规）和规范性需求（策略框架的自主性）的统一。

Method: 提出流程安全带机制，在确定性工作流引擎外包裹策略治理的代理层；开发任务-决策-流程（TDF）模型，定义数据模式和执行语义；设计三类策略治理的代理：TaskAgent（知识密集任务执行）、DecisionAgent（网关路由决策）、FlowAgent（运行时流程适应）；通过钩子机制拦截控制点，使代理在流程FRAME策略框架内进行推理；实现CUGA FLO系统进行验证。

Result: 成功实现了流程安全带机制，通过TDF模型和CUGA FLO系统，在贷款审批工作流案例中验证了三类代理类型和钩子驱动的监管覆盖功能，证明了该机制能够在保留底层引擎结构权威的同时，实现代理层的推理、适应和监督能力。

Conclusion: 流程安全带创新性地实现了命令性需求与规范性需求的协调统一：通过确定性工作流执行保证结构合规，通过在指定控制点调用策略框架的代理自主性实现灵活适应，为传统工作流向Agentic BPM的升级提供了可行路径，无需替换现有引擎基础设施。

Abstract: We introduce the process harness, a new mechanism for uplifting legacy workflows into Agentic Business Process Management (Agentic BPM) without replacing the underlying workflow engine. A process harness places a policy-governed agentic layer around a deterministic workflow engine, intercepting designated control points to contribute reasoning, adaptation, and oversight while the engine retains structural authority over the process. To define the process harness rigorously, we develop the Task-Decision-Flow (TDF) model, specifying both its data schema and its execution semantics. TDF decomposes LLM reasoning across three policy-governed agent types: a TaskAgent for knowledge-intensive task execution, a DecisionAgent for per-case gateway routing, and a FlowAgent that governs runtime flow adaptation through a principled hook mechanism. Each agent reasons within an explicit policy drawn from the process FRAME, the aggregate policy set governing all LLM calls in the system. We then present CUGA FLO as the design and implementation realization of the TDF model, and demonstrate it on a loan approval workflow that exercises all three agent types and hook-driven regulatory override. The process harness uniquely reconciles imperative requirements, realized through deterministic workflow execution that enforces structural compliance, with normative requirements, realized through policy-framed agentic autonomy invoked at designated control points wherever the process demands it.

</details>


### [183] [Vulnerability of Natural Language Classifiers to Evolutionary Generated Adversarial Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27215)
*Manjinder Singh,Alexander E. I. Brownlee,Mohamed Elawady*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GAversary，一种基于遗传算法的黑盒对抗攻击方法，通过GloVe嵌入进行词替换来生成对抗样本，在降低模型准确率方面显著优于BAE和A2T方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在NLP领域易受对抗攻击，现有攻击方法通常需要访问模型内部结构。针对这一问题，本文旨在开发一种仅依赖模型输出logit值的黑盒攻击方法，既能有效降低目标模型准确率，又能保持对抗样本的语义相似性。

Method: 提出GAversary混合遗传算法框架，将目标模型视为黑盒，仅使用模型输出的logit值指导搜索过程。该方法的核心创新在于使用GloVe词嵌入来指导变异操作中的词替换，以提高对抗样本的语义相似性，同时采用遗传算法的进化策略进行优化搜索。

Result: 在多个基准数据集和知名目标模型上的实验表明，GAversary显著降低了目标模型的测试准确率。最佳情况下，将模型准确率从76.8%降至5.8%，而对比方法BAE仅降至27.6%。代价是GAversary替换的单词数量约为其他方法的近两倍，语义相似度略低于原始文本，运行时间增加约5%。

Conclusion: GAversary证明了遗传算法在生成NLP对抗攻击方面的有效性，即使在黑盒设置下也能取得优越的攻击效果。该方法为评估NLP模型鲁棒性提供了有效工具，同时揭示了准确率下降与语义保持、计算效率之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Deep learning models have achieved impressive performance across various fields but remain vulnerable to adversarial inputs, particularly in NLP, where such attacks can have significant real-world consequences. Adversarial attacks often involve small, semantically similar token replacements to fool NLP models, and recent methods have become more precise by targeting specific vulnerable words, often by exploiting some level of access to the model's internal structure. This paper proposes GAversary, a hybrid Genetic Algorithm (GA) to generate adversarial attacks on natural language models. The GA is able to treat the target model as a black box, requiring only the logit value output by the model to guide the search. GAversary differs from GAs previously proposed for this problem by using GloVe embeddings to propose word replacements (the mutation operator) to improve the semantic similarity of the adversarial examples. GAversary is applied to several benchmark data sets and well-known target models. GAversary is able to substantially reduce the target model's accuracy on test data compared to the BAE and A2T attacks compared against (in the best case, reducing a 76.8% accuracy to 5.8%, compared to BAE's 27.6%). The trade-off is that GAversary perturbs just under twice as many words as the other two methods, with a slightly lower semantic similarity to the original text and around a 5% increase in run-time.

</details>


### [184] [Ask, Don't Judge: Binary Questions for Interpretable LLM Evaluation and Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27226)
*Sangwoo Cho,Kushal Chawla,Pengshan Cai,Zefang Liu,Chenyang Zhu,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了BINEVAL框架，通过将评估标准分解为原子级二元问题并聚合为可解释的多维评分，实现了与人类判断相当或更优的相关性，同时避免了以往LLM评估器的天花板效应，并支持迭代式提示优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM输出评估存在三大瓶颈：人工评估昂贵且缓慢、词汇指标与开放生成任务的人类判断相关性差、整体式LLM评估器产生的评分难以调试。现有评估方法缺乏透明度和可诊断性，无法有效区分边界样本与明显缺陷样本。

Method: BINEVAL框架将评估标准分解为原子级二元问题。给定任务提示，元提示生成细粒度评估问题，LLM对每个输出独立回答这些问题，产生透明的问题级反馈与校准的整体评分。该分解方法使评估更易于检查、诊断，并可直接用于提示改进，实现任务无关、无需训练且可解释的评估。

Result: 在SummEval、Topical-Chat和QAGS数据集上，BINEVAL匹配或超越UniEval和G-Eval等强基线，在QAGS等事实一致性基准上表现尤为突出。该方法更好地匹配人类评分分布，避免了以往LLM评估器常见的天花板效应，能更好地区分边界样本与明显缺陷样本。问题级反馈支持迭代式提示优化，在摘要任务和IFBench生成任务的自更新与跨模型更新设置下均有效。

Conclusion: BINEVAL成功提供了一个任务无关、无需训练、可解释的评估框架，结合了强经验性能与实用的诊断和优化价值，有效解决了LLM输出评估中的透明度、可诊断性和评分区分度问题。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM outputs remains a major bottleneck in NLP: human evaluation is expensive and slow, lexical metrics correlate poorly with human judgments on open-ended generation, and holistic LLM judges often produce opaque scores that are hard to debug. We propose BINEVAL, a framework that decomposes evaluation criteria into atomic binary questions and aggregates the resulting verdicts into interpretable, multi-dimensional scores. Given a task prompt, a meta-prompt generates fine-grained evaluation questions, and an LLM answers them independently for each output, yielding transparent question-level feedback together with calibrated overall scores. This decomposition makes evaluation easier to inspect, easier to diagnose, and directly usable for prompt improvement. Across SummEval, Topical-Chat, and QAGS, BINEVAL matches or outperforms strong baselines including UniEval and G-Eval, with especially strong results on factual consistency benchmarks such as QAGS. Beyond competitive correlation with human judgments, BINEVAL better matches human score distributions and avoids the ceiling effects common in prior LLM judges, leading to better discrimination between borderline and clearly flawed outputs. We further show that the same question-level feedback supports iterative prompt optimization, improving evaluator prompts on summarization and generation prompts on IFBench under both self-update and cross-model update settings. Overall, BINEVAL provides a task-agnostic, training-free, and interpretable evaluation framework that combines strong empirical performance with practical diagnostic and optimization value.

</details>


### [185] [EO-WM: A Physically Informed World Model for Probabilistic Earth Observation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27277)
*Junwei Luo,Shuai Yuan,Zhenya Yang,Yansheng Li,Zhe Liu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EO-WM视频扩散Transformer模型用于地球观测预测，通过物理启发的条件框架处理天气驱动的不确定性，在极端天气响应方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未能充分处理地球观测预测中的部分观测、天气驱动的世界建模问题：确定性模型忽略了不确定性，扩散方法未充分区分天气条件信号，现有基准主要关注重建精度而非天气响应的正确性。

Method: 提出EO-WM视频扩散Transformer，引入物理启发的条件框架，将气象强迫分解为气候基线、天气异常和累积物理胁迫信号，通过不同条件路径分离基线和异常，随时间累积异常强迫以捕捉持续热干旱胁迫。同时提出两个诊断基准：极端夏季基准用于评估极端天气下植被退化的严重性感知预测，季节配对基准用于测试天气强迫变化下的响应保真度。

Result: EO-WM在预测归一化植被指数(NDVI)下降幅度的误差上相对减少5.63%，方向命中率相对提高7.80%，同时在标准像素级指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: EO-WM通过物理启发的条件框架有效捕捉了天气驱动的不确定性，新提出的诊断基准能够评估模型对天气变化的响应能力，模型和基准已开源。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) forecasting aims to predict future Earth surface dynamics from satellite observations under changing meteorological conditions. In this paper, we view this task as a partially observed, weather-driven world modeling problem, in which weather acts as a conditioning signal, while forecasting remains uncertain due to sparse observations and unobserved land-surface states. However, existing methods do not fully capture this setting: deterministic models collapse uncertainty into a single future prediction, while diffusion-based methods typically treat weather variables as undifferentiated conditioning signals, and existing benchmarks focus mainly on reconstruction accuracy rather than whether forecasts respond correctly to changed weather forcing.We introduce EO-WM, a video diffusion transformer for multispectral EO forecasting. EO-WM incorporates a physically informed conditioning framework that represents meteorological forcing through a climatological baseline, weather anomalies, and cumulative physical stress signals. Specifically, it separates baseline and anomaly through distinct conditioning pathways, and accumulates anomalous forcing over time to capture sustained heat and drought stress. To evaluate weather-response behavior beyond standard metrics, we introduce two diagnostic benchmarks: an Extreme Summer Benchmark for severity-aware prediction of vegetation degradation under extreme weather, and a Seasonal Matched-Pair Benchmark for testing response fidelity under changed weather forcing. Experiments show that EO-WM reduces the error in predicted Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI) decline amplitude by a relative 5.63% and improves directional hit rate by a relative 7.80%, while remaining competitive on standard pixel-level metrics. The benchmarks and model will be made open-source at https://github.com/Luo-Z13/EO-WM.

</details>


### [186] [Simulation-based inference for rapid Bayesian parameter estimation in epidemiological models: a comparison with MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27286)
*Alina Bazarova,Johann Fredrik Jadebeck,Henrik Zunker,Carolina J. Klett-Tammen,Torben Heinsohn,Wolfgang Wiechert,Katharina Noeh,Stefan Kesselheim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出使用基于神经后验估计的仿真推断(SBI)作为马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛(MCMC)的替代方案，用于贝叶斯校准机制性SECIR流行病学模型，在保持后验估计准确性的同时，将计算时间从数千秒降至数十至数百秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制性流行病学模型的贝叶斯校准通常使用MCMC方法，但对于高维非线性系统和重复的近实时分析，MCMC计算成本高昂且耗时，亟需一种可扩展的替代方案来支持快速疫情分析和公共卫生决策。

Method: 研究采用基于神经后验估计的仿真推断(SBI)方法，利用德国2020年COVID-19重症监护病房(ICU)占用数据，对机制性SECIR流行病学模型进行贝叶斯校准。研究设计了31天推断窗口和更具挑战性的201天重建问题（包含多个传播变化点），通过Wasserstein距离和Kullback-Leibler散度及后验预测检验定量评估SBI与MCMC的后验一致性。

Result: 在31天推断窗口中，SBI恢复的后验分布与MCMC高度一致，且准确重现观测的ICU轨迹；在201天场景中，尽管不确定性增加，SBI仍保持了主导后验结构。计算效率方面，31天问题MCMC需约1000秒，SBI仅需60-70秒（单GPU）；201天问题MCMC超过19,000秒，SBI平均仅需157秒。

Conclusion: SBI为机制性流行病学模型的贝叶斯校准提供了快速且计算高效的框架，能够支持重复的近实时推断和快速疫情分析，是MCMC方法的有效可扩展替代方案。

Abstract: Mechanistic epidemiological models are widely used to support infectious disease forecasting and public-health decision making. Bayesian calibration of such models is commonly performed using Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC), which can become computationally expensive for high-dimensional nonlinear systems and repeated near-real-time analyses. Here, we investigate simulation-based inference (SBI) using neural posterior estimation as a scalable alternative for Bayesian calibration of a mechanistic SECIR epidemiological model using COVID-19 intensive care unit (ICU) occupancy data from Germany during 2020. We compared SBI and MCMC across multiple epidemic phases using both 31-day inference windows and a substantially more challenging 201-day reconstruction problem involving multiple transmission change points. Posterior agreement was evaluated quantitatively using Wasserstein distances and Kullback-Leibler divergences together with posterior predictive checks. Across the 31-day windows, SBI recovered posterior distributions in strong agreement with MCMC while accurately reproducing observed ICU trajectories. In the 201-day setting, SBI preserved the dominant posterior structure despite increased uncertainty. SBI, by combining CPU and GPU resources, substantially reduced computational runtime compared with MCMC, which was restricted to running on CPUs. Whereas MCMC required approximately 1000 seconds for the 31-day inference problems, SBI achieved comparable posterior and predictive performance in approximately 60-70 seconds on a single GPU. For the 201-day inference problem, SBI required an average of 157 seconds, while the MCMC runs took over 19,000 seconds. Our results demonstrate that SBI provides a rapid and computationally efficient framework for Bayesian calibration of mechanistic epidemiological models, supporting repeated near-real-time inference and rapid outbreak analysis.

</details>


### [187] [Prompt Injection in Automated Résumé Screening with Large Language Models: Single and Multi-Injection Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27287)
*Preet Baxi,Jiannan Xu,Jane Yi Jiang,Stefanus Jasin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了自动简历筛选中的提示注入攻击，发现当操纵行为稀少且候选人质量相近时，攻击效果显著；但随着操纵普及化，效果迅速衰减，且在质量差异较大的情况下可能引发公平性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在招聘筛选中的应用日益广泛，求职者具有操纵算法系统的动机。研究者旨在探究提示注入（即在简历中添加微妙的自我推销文本而不引入新资质）如何影响LLM的评估结果，以及这种操纵策略的有效性和潜在公平性问题。

Method: 采用对照实验方法，在不同实验条件下测试提示注入策略的有效性：1）控制简历质量同质性与异质性；2）调节实施提示注入的候选人数量；3）评估LLM对候选人排名的变化。通过系统性的实验设计，量化分析操纵策略在不同场景下的成功率和影响程度。

Result: 1）当简历质量同质且少数候选人使用提示注入时，排名提升效果显著；2）随着操纵行为普及，效果迅速下降，当操纵普遍化时完全失效；3）在质量异质场景中，虽然平均效果较弱，但偶尔能使低质量候选人超越高质量候选人；4）系统在操纵稀少且质量差异微小时最为脆弱。

Conclusion: LLM简历筛选系统存在明显的操纵漏洞，特别是在早期采用策略时效果最强，但随着策略普及会产生"公地悲剧"式的效果崩溃。这凸显了LLM招聘系统的公平性风险，需要在系统设计中考虑对抗性操纵的防御机制。研究代码已开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to screen and rank job applicants, creating incentives for candidates to strategically manipulate algorithmic hiring systems. We study prompt injection in automated résumé screening, defined as subtle self-promotional text that introduces no new qualifications but is designed to influence LLM evaluations. Using controlled experiments, we show that prompt injection reliably improves applicant rankings when résumé quality is homogeneous and few candidates inject. However, its effectiveness rapidly diminishes as more candidates inject, collapsing when manipulation becomes widespread. When candidate quality is heterogeneous, prompt injection is less effective on average, but can occasionally allow lower-quality candidates to outrank higher-quality ones, raising fairness concerns. Overall, LLM-based screening is most vulnerable when manipulation is rare and candidate quality differences are small. Code and resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/preetb1199/Prompt_Injection_ACL26

</details>


### [188] [When Does Combining Language Models Help? A Co-Failure Ceiling on Routing, Voting, and Mixture-of-Agents Across 67 Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27288)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多模型LLM系统的性能提升受限于所有模型同时出错的概率（β），当β=0.052-0.127时，组合策略难以超越单一最佳模型，收益源于模型错误的差异性而非模型数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明多模型系统（如路由、投票、级联等）能超越单模型准确率，但缺乏对其增益上限的理论约束分析。研究旨在揭示并量化这一被忽视的性能瓶颈。

Method: 推导出准确率上限公式：1-β（β为所有模型在同一查询上全部错误的比率），证明传统指标ρ（平均成对错误相关性）无法识别β；使用Clopper-Pearson界进行有限样本认证；在67个模型（21个提供商）上验证，采用四分相关校准的单因子模型和Gaussian copula分析。

Result: 开放式数学任务中观测β=0.052，而67模型Gaussian copula预测仅0.023，低估约2.5倍（90%CI:1.7-3.4）；代码任务β=0.079；GPQA-Diamond自由回答形式β=0.127；在可验证任务中，组合模型难以在不依赖强查询级路由信号时超越单模型最佳表现。

Conclusion: 多模型系统的增益根本上受限于所有模型同时失败的概率β，收益来源于模型失败的差异性而非模型数量增加；低ρ异质集成优于高ρ Self-MoA，但实际任务中组合策略很少能超越单一最佳模型。

Abstract: Multi-model LLM systems such as routing, voting, cascades, fusion, and mixture-of-agents are used to beat single-model accuracy. We show that their gain is capped by a quantity the field rarely reports. For any policy whose output is one member model answer, accuracy cannot exceed one minus beta, where beta is the rate at which every model is wrong on the same query. In contrast, the usual diagnostic, average pairwise error correlation rho, cannot identify beta: error laws with identical marginals and pairwise correlations can have different all-wrong rates. A Clopper-Pearson bound on beta gives a finite-sample certificate on the largest gain any router, vote, or cascade could deliver before training a router.
  Across 67 models from 21 providers, a tetrachoric-calibrated single-factor model still underprices the all-wrong tail: on open-ended mathematics, observed beta is 0.052 versus 0.023 under the full 67-model Gaussian copula, about 2.5 times underpricing, with 90 percent CI 1.7 to 3.4 and k equals 17. The effect recurs on execution-graded code, where beta is 0.079. Re-asking the same GPQA-Diamond questions in free-response rather than multiple-choice form reopens the tail, with beta 0.127 and a five-judge panel with kappa 0.73 to 0.92, locating co-failure in answer format rather than subject. At matched quality, low-rho heterogeneous ensembles beat high-rho Self-MoA, but on checkable tasks in our pool, combining models rarely beats the single best model without a strong query-level routing signal. Gains come from models failing on different questions, not from adding more models.

</details>


### [189] [Language-Based Digital Twins for Elderly Cognitive Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27334)
*Mohammad Mehdi Hosseini,Mohammad H. Mahoor,Hiroko H. Dodge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的语言数字孪生框架，用于模拟老年人的对话行为，实现了对轻度认知障碍（MCI）的早期检测和持续监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生是个性化医疗的新兴范式，能够对个体行为和健康轨迹进行建模。在认知健康领域，轻度认知障碍（MCI）的早期检测仍具有挑战性，而语言和对话模式可作为非侵入性生物标志物。因此需要一种可扩展、非侵入性的方法来实现个性化的持续认知健康监测。

Method: 提出了一种基于语言的大语言模型数字孪生框架，通过整合文体特征（stylometric cues）和上下文元数据来模拟老年人的对话行为。为评估保真度和认知一致性，引入了多头条件变分自编码器（cVAE），联合测量重建质量并预测认知评分。

Result: 在I-CONECT数据集上的实验表明，该数字孪生框架能够保持身份特定特征，其重建质量和MoCA预测误差与真实数据相当，并且优于基线GPT生成的响应。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，基于语言的数字孪生作为一种可扩展且非侵入性的方法，在个性化和持续认知健康监测方面具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Digital twins have emerged as a promising paradigm for personalized healthcare, enabling modeling of individual behavior and health trajectories. In cognitive health, early detection of Mild Cognitive Impairment (MCI) remains challenging, where language and conversational patterns serve as non-invasive biomarkers. In this work, we propose a language-based digital twin framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to mimic the conversational behavior of elderly individuals by incorporating stylometric cues and contextual metadata. To evaluate fidelity and cognitive consistency, we introduce a multi-head conditional variational autoencoder (cVAE) that jointly measures reconstruction quality and predicts cognitive scores. Experiments on the I-CONECT dataset show that the digital twin preserves identity-specific characteristics and achieves reconstruction and MoCA prediction errors comparable to real data, while outperforming baseline GPT-generated responses. These results highlight the potential of language-based digital twins as a scalable and non-invasive approach for personalized and continuous cognitive health monitoring.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Hot AI in Cold Space: Thermal-Crosstalk-Aware Scheduling for Sustainable Orbital AI Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26150)
*Shuyi Chen,Zhengchang Hua,Nikos Tziritas,Georgios Theodoropoulos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对轨道数据中心(ODCs)在分布式大语言模型训练中面临的热-邻近悖论问题，提出热感知负载均衡(TLB)框架，通过动态迁移工作负载至最冷单元来解决热瓶颈，恢复MFU并延长硬件寿命，实现轨道AI的可持续发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面AI训练面临不可持续的能源与水危机，轨道数据中心作为零运营碳替代方案被提出。然而，分布式大语言模型训练所需的低于10μs通信延迟迫使ODCs进入极端物理密度，引发关键的热-邻近悖论：单体结构或近距集群遭受强烈的热-流体串扰(共享冷却回路中的热陷阱)和热-辐射串扰(相互加热阻断深空冷却辐射器)，导致热节流降低训练吞吐量、热疲劳缩短硬件寿命，产生过早的空间电子垃圾。

Method: 提出热感知异质性论题(Thermal-Aware Heterogeneity Thesis)，将空间冷却差异作为主要资源管理维度；基于此引入热-负载均衡(Thermal-Load Balancing, TLB)软件框架，根据瞬时流体温度或吸收辐射动态迁移LLM工作负载至最冷的可用单元。

Result: 分析表明TLB能够解决热瓶颈，恢复模型浮点利用率(MFU)，同时降低物理热应力，延长轨道硬件的运行寿命，从而分摊火箭发射的巨大隐含碳。

Conclusion: TLB为扩展轨道AI而不加速电子垃圾产生提供了必要路径，延长轨道硬件运行寿命对于实现轨道AI真正可持续至关重要。

Abstract: Terrestrial AI training faces an unsustainable energy and water crisis, positioning Orbital Data Centers (ODCs) as a "zero operational carbon" alternative. However, the sub-$10μ\text{s}$ communication latency required for distributed Large Language Model (LLM) training forces ODCs into extreme physical density, triggering a critical "Proximity-Thermal Paradox." As these high-density systems scale into Monolithic Structures or Proximity Swarms, they suffer from intense thermal-fluid crosstalk (heat traps in shared cooling loops) and thermal-radiative crosstalk (mutual heating that blocks deep-space cooling radiators). If left unmitigated, this persistent heat stagnation not only triggers severe thermal throttling that degrades training throughput, but also induces severe thermal fatigue, drastically shortening hardware lifespans and generating premature space e-waste. To make orbital AI truly sustainable, this position paper challenges traditional uniform load-sharing. We propose the Thermal-Aware Heterogeneity Thesis, which treats spatial cooling variances as a primary resource management dimension. Building on this, we introduce Thermal-Load Balancing (TLB), a software framework that dynamically migrates LLM workloads to the coolest available units based on instantaneous fluid temperatures or absorbed radiation. Our analysis demonstrates that TLB resolves thermal bottlenecks to restore Model Flops Utilization (MFU), while simultaneously reducing physical thermal stress. Extending the operational lifespan of orbital hardware is crucial to amortize the massive embodied carbon of rocket launches, outlining a necessary pathway to scale orbital AI without accelerating e-waste.

</details>


### [191] [FinWhale: An Optimally Resilient Two-Round Terminating DAG Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26292)
*Razya Ladelsky,Roy Friedman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了FinWhale，首个支持两轮消息延迟快速路径的DAG拜占庭容错协议，将最优延迟快速路径集成到 uncertified DAG 共识中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DAG拜占庭容错协议虽然能提供高吞吐量，但仍需至少三轮消息延迟才能提交决策；而传统快速路径协议虽能在理想条件下实现最优的两轮延迟，却难以与DAG结构兼容。因此，需要一种能将快速路径集成到DAG共识中的解决方案。

Method: 基于Mysteti协议进行扩展，设计了一种新颖的快速路径提交机制，使其能与原有的慢速路径规则安全共存；引入基于快速路径证据区块的新型提交结构，使验证者能够在不同本地DAG视图间保持一致性，从而安全地结合快速路径和慢速路径推理。

Result: FinWhale在部分同步模型下运行，验证者数量n=3f+2p-1，达到了快速拜占庭共识的已知下界；协议容忍最多f个拜占庭故障，当快速路径期间最多p个验证者失效时可实现快速终止（1≤p≤f）。实验证明最优延迟快速路径可成功集成到uncertified DAG共识协议中。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将最优延迟快速路径集成到uncertified DAG共识协议是可行的，为DAG拜占庭容错协议提供了兼具高吞吐量和低延迟的解决方案。

Abstract: DAG based Byzantine Fault Tolerant protocols provide high throughput consensus under partial synchrony but existing DAG protocols still require at least three message delays to commit decisions. In contrast fast path Byzantine Fault Tolerant protocols can achieve optimal two message delay termination under favorable conditions though they do not naturally extend to DAGs.
  We present FinWhale the first DAG based Byzantine Fault Tolerant protocol with a two message delay fast path. FinWhale extends Mysticeti with a novel fast path commit mechanism that safely coexists with the protocol's original slow path rules. To preserve safety across different local DAG views we introduce new commit structures based on fast path evidence blocks enabling validators to combine fast path and slow path reasoning consistently.
  FinWhale operates in the partially synchronous model with n equals three f plus two p minus one validators matching the known lower bound for fast Byzantine consensus. The protocol tolerates up to f Byzantine faults and achieves fast termination whenever at most p validators fail during the fast path where p is between one and f. Our results show that optimal latency fast paths can be integrated into uncertified DAG consensus protocols.

</details>


### [192] [A Distributed Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm Simulator for Engineering Design Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26297)
*Ali Rajabi,Milad Hasanzadeh,Amin Kargarian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个与Qiskit兼容的分布式量子近似优化算法（DQAOA）模拟器，用于解决工程设计和决策应用中的二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）问题，支持单QPU和分布式多QPU执行模式，并提供了图形用户界面和运行时优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计和决策应用中存在大量QUBO问题需要求解，但缺乏可重用的模拟器来比较不同QAOA执行模式下的求解性能，因此需要开发一个支持单体和分布式QAOA的统一模拟平台。

Method: 开发开源Qiskit兼容模拟器，支持单体QAOA（单QPU）和分布式QAOA（多QPU）两种执行模式；工作流程包括QUBO模型规范化、映射到代价哈密顿量、跨QPU变量分配、识别局部和跨QPU耦合、构建相应量子电路；引入参数化电路重用、固定深度目标重用、批量评估和并行多起点执行等运行时优化技术；提供Streamlit图形用户界面用于实例输入、配置、执行和可视化。

Result: 在独立QUBO基准测试和发电机组组合应用中验证了模拟器；在机组组合案例中，暴力搜索、单体QAOA和分布式QAOA均恢复相同的组合比特串和运行成本；多案例研究表明模拟器在最优比特串和成本方面与经典单体QAOA参考结果一致；阶段性运行时分析显示各实现阶段运行时间显著减少，但分布式QAOA因跨QPU耦合需要远程操作而计算开销更大。

Conclusion: 该模拟器成功实现了对QUBO问题的单体和分布式QAOA求解，验证了分布式QAOA与经典方法的一致性，通过运行时优化技术显著提升了效率，为工程设计和决策应用中的量子优化问题求解提供了有效工具。

Abstract: This paper presents a Qiskit-compatible distributed quantum approximate optimization algorithm (DQAOA) simulator for quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problems arising in engineering design and decision applications. The open-source simulator is available through the RAISE LAB website and GitHub repository, with README documentation for installation, input formatting, configurable parameters, and example workflows. The package addresses the need for a reusable simulator that can solve and compare QUBO instances across different QAOA execution modes. It supports monolithic QAOA on a single quantum processing unit (QPU) and distributed QAOA across a user-specified number of QPUs with configurable capacities. The workflow canonicalizes the QUBO model, maps it to a cost Hamiltonian, allocates variables across QPUs, identifies local and cross-QPU couplings, and constructs the corresponding circuits. Runtime optimizations, including parameterized circuit reuse, objective reuse at fixed depth, batched evaluations, and parallel multi-start execution, reduce repeated overhead. A Streamlit graphical user interface is also provided for entering or uploading QUBO instances, configuring solver settings, running selected modes, and visualizing solution-quality metrics without editing Python scripts. The package is demonstrated on standalone QUBO benchmarks and a power generation unit commitment application. In the unit commitment case, brute force, monolithic QAOA, and distributed QAOA recover the same commitment bitstring and operating cost. Across multiple case studies, the simulator produces results consistent with classical monolithic QAOA references in terms of optimal bitstrings and costs. Staged runtime analysis shows substantial runtime reduction across implementation stages, while distributed QAOA remains more demanding because cross-QPU couplings require remote operations.

</details>


### [193] [Priceless: An examination of Serverless Functions-as-a-Service (FaaS) pricing models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26308)
*Nnamdi Ekwe-Ekwe*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文分析了主流FaaS提供商的功能与定价模型，发现AWS Lambda成本最低，Azure Functions最昂贵，且存在显著的跨提供商和跨区域价格差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着FaaS服务在过去十年中快速发展，各提供商引入了许多新功能，但也带来了多样化且复杂的定价模型。缺乏对这些定价模型的系统性分析和比较，用户难以做出成本最优的选择。

Method: 提出Priceless框架，系统梳理当前FaaS服务的功能和定价模型，并在AWS Lambda、Microsoft Azure Functions和Google Cloud Functions三个平台上运行示例工作负载，进行对比性价格分析。

Result: 发现不同提供商之间以及同一提供商的不同区域之间存在显著的成本差异。AWS Lambda在运行函数方面总体成本最低，而Microsoft Azure Functions在相同工作负载下成本最高。

Conclusion: FaaS服务的定价模型复杂且差异显著，用户在选择提供商和部署区域时应仔细考虑成本因素，本研究为用户提供了重要的决策参考。

Abstract: Serverless Functions-as-a-Service providers have grown in their offering since inception a decade ago, with a myriad of new functionalities offered to end-users. These new features have also brought new, varied and at times complex pricing models that differ between providers. In this paper, we present Priceless, a detailed examination of the current state of the art of FaaS features, and their pricing models. We then perform a comparative price analysis of running example workloads across AWS Lambda, Microsoft Azure Functions and Google Cloud Functions. Our work finds significant cost differences both cross-provider, but also, cross-region within the provider. We find that AWS is the cheapest overall to run functions on, with Microsoft Azure being the most expensive for equivalent workloads.

</details>


### [194] [Moebius: Serving Mixture-of-Expert Models with Seamless Runtime Parallelism Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26607)
*Shaoyu Wang,Yizhuo Liang,Jaeyong Song,Chong Li,Seo Jin Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Moebius是一个MoE模型服务系统，能在运行时动态切换专家并行(EP)和张量并行(TP)，无需重启引擎或丢弃进行中的请求，在各工作负载下均能达到最优静态并行策略的性能，且内存开销仅2.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型服务中，TP在低并发时更快而EP在高并发时更优，但生产工作负载（如在线服务的突发请求、RL采样的长尾效应）会频繁跨越此边界。固定单一并行策略会导致在跨越边界时性能损失。

Method: 利用EP和TP是对同一模型的不同布局这一洞见（计算相同函数、使用字节相同的专家权重和KV缓存），通过仅迁移所有权变更的切片实现并行策略切换。系统在固定地址处重新分片权重和KV缓存，使用融合GPU间传输内核，保留两种并行布局的运行时状态，在解码步之间完成切换。

Result: 在8×H200 GPU上服务Qwen3-235B-A22B模型时，Moebius在每个操作点都能匹敌最优静态并行策略，在RL采样任务中性能提升1.16-1.25倍；切换耗时215-434毫秒，内存开销仅2.4%。

Conclusion: Moebius证明了MoE模型服务中动态并行策略切换的可行性，通过统一EP和TP为同一模型的不同布局并优化迁移成本，实现了生产工作负载下的自适应高性能服务。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) to hundreds of billions of parameters. Serving a single MoE model requires multiple GPUs operating in parallel, typically through tensor parallelism (TP) or expert parallelism (EP). The optimal choice depends on the number of in-flight requests: TP is faster at low concurrency, whereas EP wins at high concurrency. Production workloads cross this boundary continually: online serving sees bursty arrivals that subside into quiet periods, and reinforcement-learning rollouts begin as a high-concurrency burst that decays into a long tail of stragglers. Pinning either layout therefore forfeits performance when the workload crosses to the other side.
  We present Moebius, a serving system that switches between EP and TP at runtime without restarting the engine or dropping in-flight requests. Our key insight is that EP and TP are two layouts of one model, not two models: they compute the same function over byte-identical expert weights and KV cache, so a switch changes only which rank owns each slice. Moving those owner-changed slices is the sole irreducible cost, and modern high-bandwidth GPU interconnects make it fast enough to do between decode steps without draining in-flight requests. Moebius preserves each parallelism's runtime resident, and reshards the single copy of expert weights and KV cache at fixed addresses with fused GPU-to-GPU transfer kernels. On 8x H200 GPUs serving Qwen3-235B-A22B, Moebius matches the better static parallelism at every operating point, and beats it on RL rollouts by 1.16-1.25x across steps. Each switch completes in 215-434 ms, and Moebius holds both layouts resident with only 2.4% memory overhead.

</details>


### [195] [Simulating Unified Tensor Resharding in heterogeneous AI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26633)
*Sumit Kumar,Sayantan Dasgupta,Kushal Mitra,Meet Dadhania,Rohan Sudhir Basugade,Praveen Tammana,Satananda Burla,Abed Mohammad Kamaluddin,Rinku Shah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Xsim，一个面向分布式大语言模型训练的异构感知模拟器，能够在异构计算和网络基础设施环境下准确预测训练时间（误差低于5%），并支持非均匀负载均衡、异构感知集合通信等关键特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI训练模拟器假设基础设施是同构的，但现实世界训练基础设施日益异构化：多模态和MoE等模型架构利用异构性提升设备利用率；公有云平台由于硬件快速演进导致同构硬件可用性有限；大型企业常部署地理分布且异构的基础设施。

Method: 提出Xsim异构感知模拟器，支持五项关键技术：(i) 通过异构设备组间的非均匀工作负载划分实现负载均衡；(ii) 通过定制环形构建和分块划分实现异构感知集合通信；(iii) 面向新兴流水线并行算法和非均匀张量重分片的可复用异构感知抽象；(iv) 支持自定义设备组和设备-并行性映射的灵活输入抽象；(v) 与NS-3和htsim可插拔集成，允许用户在模拟保真度与性能可扩展性之间权衡。

Result: Xsim能够准确预测异构部署的训练时间，在大多数异构数据并行/张量并行配置下误差小于5%，在流水线并行通信建模中误差约2%。系统暴露了可操作的指标，如流水线气泡时间和落后者等待时间。

Conclusion: Xsim成功解决了现有模拟器无法处理异构基础设施的问题，为分布式大语言模型训练在异构环境下的性能预测和分析提供了准确、灵活的工具，对实际部署具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: State-of-the-art AI training simulators assume homogeneous compute and network infrastructure. However, real-world training infrastructure is becoming increasingly heterogeneous since: (a) Model architectures such as multimodal and MoE exploit heterogeneity to improve device utilization, (b) Public cloud platforms often provide limited availability of homogeneous hardware due to fast hardware evolution, and (c) Large enterprises frequently deploy geographically distributed infrastructure that is both diverse and heterogeneous. In this paper, we present Xsim, a heterogeneity-aware simulator for distributed LLM training. Xsim supports: (i) Load balancing through non-uniform workload partitioning across heterogeneous device groups, (ii) Heterogeneity-aware collective communication via customized ring construction and chunk partitioning, (iii) Reusable heterogeneity-aware abstractions for emerging pipeline-parallel algorithms and non-uniform tensor resharding technique, (iv) Flexible input abstractions for specifying deployment plans with custom device groups and custom device-to-parallelism mappings, and (v) Pluggable integration with NS-3 and htsim, allowing users to trade off simulation fidelity for performance and scalability. Our evaluation demonstrates that Xsim accurately predicts training time for real-world heterogeneous deployments, with an error of less than 5% across most heterogeneous data-parallel/tensor-parallel configurations and around 2% error with pipeline-parallel communication modeling. We expose actionable metrics such as pipeline bubble time and straggler waiting time.

</details>


### [196] [RolloutPipe: Overlapping Pipelined Rollout and Training in Disaggregated On-Policy LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26997)
*Rongjian Chen,Jianmin Hu,Kejiang Ye,Minxian Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出RolloutPipe框架，通过complete-group pipelining和frontier-group dispatch技术，在 disaggregated RLVR系统中实现训练与rollout的流水线重叠，相比SOTA系统Slime将rollout到训练结束时间缩短30.7%-42.3%，trainer等待比例降低37%-76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同步on-policy GRPO RLVR系统需等待整个rollout完成才能开始训练，导致trainer GPU池空闲；而异步RL流水线虽能重叠两阶段，却以使用陈旧数据训练为代价，影响训练正确性。

Method: 提出RolloutPipe框架，包含两项核心技术：(1) Complete-group pipelining (CGP)：在group materialization完成后立即将可训练的complete group按FIFO调度至trainer；(2) Frontier-group dispatch (FGD)：rollout节点的准入策略，优先处理形成下一训练batch所需的frontier groups，使trainer-ready groups更早、更稳定到达。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B模型上，跨四个推理和科学benchmark、十二种rollout设置进行评估：rollout到训练结束时间缩短30.7%-42.3%，trainer等待比例降低37%-76%（相比Slime系统）。

Conclusion: RolloutPipe成功在保持on-policy正确性的前提下，实现训练与rollout的流水线重叠，显著提升了disaggregated RLVR系统的资源利用效率和训练速度。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) post-training for reasoning increasingly relies on reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), where models learn from ground-truth feedback on mathematical, logical, and scientific tasks. To enable flexible resource allocation and support heterogeneous training setups, modern RLVR systems adopt disaggregated architectures that decouple rollout generation and policy training across independent GPU pools. However, existing synchronous on-policy GRPO (Group Relative Policy Optimization) RLVR systems finish an entire rollout before starting training, leaving the trainer GPU pool idle while rollout is still ongoing. Asynchronous RL pipelines overlap the two stages, but at the cost of training on stale data. To address these challenges, we propose RolloutPipe, a post-training framework for disaggregated RLVR systems, which turns the fixed-weight rollout into a complete-group pipeline where trainable groups move to the trainer while later groups are still being generated. RolloutPipe achieves this through two techniques including complete-group pipelining (CGP) and frontier-group dispatch (FGD). CGP dispatches each trainable complete group to the trainer FIFO as soon as group materialization finishes, and FGD is an admission policy on the Rollout node that first admits requests for the frontier groups needed to form the next training batch, so that trainer-ready groups arrive earlier and more steadily. The design starts training before the rollout completes while maintaining on-policy correctness. Evaluated on Qwen3-1.7B across four reasoning and science benchmarks and twelve rollout settings, RolloutPipe shortens the rollout-to-train-end time by 30.7%-42.3%, and lowers the trainer waiting ratio by 37%-76% compared to Slime, a state-of-the-art rollout and training system.

</details>


### [197] [DMuon: Efficient Distributed Muon Training with Near-Adam Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27153)
*Vincent Chen,Starrick Liu,Regis Cheng,Dance Yang,Shalfun Li,Ryan Yu,Lucy Liang,Hang Su,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了DMuon，一种分布式Muon优化器实现，将优化器步骤速度提升6.85x-163.00x，端到端训练速度提升1.48x-3.01x，使矩阵正交化优化器的性能接近AdamW水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式训练基础设施基于元素级优化器设计，无法有效支持Muon等矩阵级优化器，后者需要耦合整个权重矩阵并进行昂贵的Newton-Schulz迭代，导致普通Muon实现的成本超过前向和反向传播的2倍。

Method: 提出DMuon，一个开源的分布式Muon实现，作为即插即用模块集成到现有训练流程中，无需框架级修改，针对矩阵级更新进行优化。

Result: 在具身基础模型和大语言模型(LLM)训练任务中，DMuon实现端到端步骤时间加速1.48x-3.01x，优化器步骤时间加速6.85x-163.00x，使每步延迟接近AdamW水平并实现高效扩展。

Conclusion: DMuon成功弥合了矩阵正交化优化器与现有分布式训练基础设施之间的差距，使Muon优化器能够在实际大规模模型训练中高效部署应用。

Abstract: Matrix-orthogonalization-based optimizers, exemplified by Muon, have demonstrated strong convergence behavior across a wide range of modern deep learning workloads. The matrix-aware updates offer a compelling alternative to conventional element-wise optimization, particularly as model architectures continue to grow in scale and heterogeneity. Yet contemporary distributed training infrastructure built around the assumption of element-wise optimizers is poorly matched to matrix-level optimizers such as Muon, whose updates couple entire weight matrices and require costly Newton-Schulz iterations. Vanilla Muon implementations incur more than 2x the cost of forward and backward passes. To close this gap, we present DMuon, an open-source distributed Muon implementation that integrates into existing training pipelines as a drop-in module, with no framework-level modifications. Across both embodied foundation model and large language model (LLM) training workloads, DMuon achieves a 1.48x-3.01x speedup in end-to-end step time and a 6.85x-163.00x speedup in optimizer-step time, bringing per-step latency to near-AdamW levels and enabling efficient scaling in our model training.

</details>


### [198] [CHAMB-GA: A Containerized HPC Scalable Microservice-Based Framework for Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27217)
*Felix Bonhoff,Thiemo Pesch,Andrea Benigni,Alexander Mitsos,Manuel Dahmen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CHAMB-GA框架，这是一个基于容器化和微服务架构的遗传算法框架，支持嵌入式仿真的大规模并行优化计算，能够在云基础设施和HPC集群之间无缝扩展，并在超过3500个CPU核心上验证了其可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于元启发式的全局优化结合长时间运行的仿真是一个计算密集型过程。现有工具链存在僵化和硬件绑定约束，阻碍了可扩展性和复杂仿真的集成。需要一种能够支持从个人计算机到分布式集群无缝过渡的解决方案，以实现跨所有计算规模的执行。

Method: 提出了CHAMB-GA（Containerized HPC scalable Microservice-Based framework for Genetic Algorithms）框架：1）采用容器化技术实现跨云基础设施和HPC集群的一致性部署；2）用户将GA算子和仿真后端分离提供；3）框架以分布式、解耦的方式运行组件，映射到独立的硬件；4）通过中央消息代理协调微服务间的异步管理器-工作者通信，并行化进化操作和适应度评估；5）支持容器编排（Kubernetes）和批处理调度并行执行（SLURM）。

Result: 通过两部分验证：1）基准研究表明在扩展到超过3500个CPU核心时开销最小，证明了框架的可扩展性；2）德国输电网高压直流（HVDC）线路调度优化案例，展示了从Kubernetes到SLURM的无缝迁移、水平和垂直组合扩展能力、以及多阶段工作流的集成。框架成功实现了可扩展性、可移植性和可重复性，同时促进了外部工具和复杂仿真的集成。

Conclusion: CHAMB-GA框架成功弥合了从个人计算机到分布式集群执行之间的差距，通过容器化和微服务架构实现了硬件解耦和灵活扩展。该框架为计算昂贵的元启发式优化问题提供了一个高效、可扩展、可移植的解决方案，特别适用于需要大规模并行计算的复杂仿真优化场景。

Abstract: Metaheuristic-based global optimization with embedded, long-running simulations is a computationally expensive process. To support various stages of development and execution, a seamless transition from personal computers to distributed clusters is desired, enabling execution across all computational scales. However, existing tool chains are often characterized by rigidity and hardware-bound constraints, which impede scalability and the integration of complex simulations. Bridging this gap, we present a containerized HPC scalable microservice-based framework for genetic algorithms with embedded simulations (CHAMB-GA). The deployment of the framework scales consistently across cloud infrastructure via container orchestration and HPC clusters via batch-scheduled parallel execution. Users provide the GA operators and simulation backend separately. The framework is designed to run these components in a distributed and decoupled manner, mapped to separate hardware. This approach ensures that the fitness evaluation and genetic operations are not managed within the same process and are utilizing distinct parts of the compute infrastructure. A central message broker coordinates asynchronous manager-worker communication between microservices, thereby parallelizing evolutionary operations and fitness evaluations. We demonstrate CHAMB-GA's scalability, portability, and reproducibility, while facilitating the integration of external tools and complex simulations on benchmark and powerflow problems. The capabilities of CHAMB-GA are validated in a two-part approach: (i) a benchmark study demonstrating minimal overhead while scaling to over 3,500 CPU cores, and (ii) a dispatch optimization of High Voltage Direct Current (HVDC) lines in the German transmission grid, showing seamless migration from Kubernetes to SLURM, combined horizontal and vertical scaling, and integration of multi-stage workflows.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Kiko: Programming Agents to Enact Interaction Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26156)
*Samuel H. Christie,Munindar P. Singh,Amit K. Chopra*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Kiko，一个基于协议的多智能体编程模型，通过决策者抽象和通信服务封装，使开发者能专注于业务逻辑，同时保证协议合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体系统的编程模型在决策制定方面缺乏良好的抽象，且未能有效连接智能体的内部决策逻辑与公共决策，导致开发困难且易出错。

Method: 提出Kiko编程模型：程序员编写一个或多个决策器(decision makers)，每个决策器从有效决策集合中选择并做出相互兼容的消息发送决策；完全抽象底层通信服务；支持实用的决策模式；提供操作语义形式化定义。

Result: 建立了Kiko的操作语义，证明Kiko智能体具有协议合规性(protocol compliant)，能够实现任何协议执行(protocol enactment)。

Conclusion: Kiko通过决策器抽象和通信服务封装，有效解决了现有模型的问题，使开发者专注于业务逻辑，同时保证了协议合规性和实现的完备性。

Abstract: Realizing a multiagent system involves implementing member agents who interact based on a protocol while making decisions in a decentralized manner. Current programming models for agents offer poor abstractions for decision making and fail to adequately bridge an agent's internal decision logic with its public decisions.
  We present Kiko, a protocol-based programming model for agents. To implement an agent, a programmer writes one or more decision makers, each of which chooses from among a set of valid decisions and makes mutually compatible decisions on what messages to send. By completely abstracting away the underlying communication service and by supporting practical decision-making patterns, Kiko enables agent developers to focus on business logic. We provide an operational semantics for Kiko and establish that Kiko agents are protocol compliant and able to realize any protocol enactment.

</details>
